# List 14

## Unit 1

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>interminable</b></td><td>/ɪnˈtɜːrmɪnəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无尽头的</td><td>an interminable meeting<br>没完没了的会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After an interminable meeting, we still had not agreed on a budget.  经过一场没完没了的会议，我们仍未就预算达成一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 continual</td><td>/kənˈtɪnjuəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>连续的，不断的</td><td>continual interruptions<br>不断的打扰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Continual delays finally forced them to cancel the trip.  接连不断的延误最终迫使他们取消行程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ceaseless</td><td>/ˈsiːslɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不停的，持续的</td><td>ceaseless noise<br>不停的噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceaseless traffic outside made it impossible for us to sleep.  外面不停的车流让我们无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 endless</td><td>/ˈendləs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无尽的，无休止的</td><td>endless complaints<br>无休止的抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The meeting felt endless, though it lasted only ninety minutes.  会议感觉没完没了，其实只开了九十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 everlasting</td><td>/ˌevərˈlæstɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，持久的</td><td>everlasting peace<br>永久的和平</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They vowed everlasting loyalty to each other.  他们发誓永远彼此忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perpetual</td><td>/pərˈpetʃuəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不断的</td><td>perpetual motion<br>永恒运动，永动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lives in perpetual fear of losing his job.  他一直生活在失业的恐惧中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 completed</td><td>/kəmˈpliːtəd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>完成的，已结束的</td><td>a completed application<br>填妥的申请表</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only completed forms will be accepted after Friday.  周五之后只接受填写完整的表格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>intermittent</b></td><td>/ˌɪntərˈmɪtənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>断断续续的</td><td>intermittent power failures<br>断断续续的停电</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Intermittent power failures made it impossible to finish the experiment that night.  断断续续的停电使当晚无法完成那项实验。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 episodic</td><td>/ˌepɪˈsɑːdɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>间歇性的；不定期的</td><td>episodic bursts of rain<br>断断续续的阵雨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Episodic bursts of rain kept interrupting the outdoor ceremony all afternoon.  断断续续的阵雨整个下午不断打断露天典礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 erratic</td><td>/ɪˈrætɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反复无常的，不规律的</td><td>erratic driving behavior<br>飘忽不定的驾驶行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His erratic driving behavior alarmed the passengers long before the police stopped him.  早在警察拦下他之前，他飘忽不定的驾驶行为就让乘客惊慌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 occasional</td><td>/əˈkeɪʒənəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偶尔的，间或的</td><td>occasional showers<br>偶有阵雨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He makes occasional visits to his hometown in autumn.  他秋天偶尔回故乡看看。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 periodical</td><td>/ˌpɪriˈɑːdɪkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>周期性的，有固定间隔的</td><td>periodical inspections<br>定期检查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The airline carries out periodical inspections of every engine and wing.  该航空公司对每台发动机和机翼进行定期检查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recurrent</td><td>/rɪˈkɜːrənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反复出现的</td><td>a recurrent theme<br>反复出现的主题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loneliness is a recurrent theme in her early poems.  孤独是她早期诗作中反复出现的主题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recurring</td><td>/rɪˈkɜːrɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>反复发生的</td><td>a recurring dream<br>反复出现的梦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He suffers from a recurring pain in his left knee.  他左膝反复疼痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 constant</td><td>/ˈkɑːnstənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不变的，恒定的；忠诚的</td><td>a constant friend<br>忠诚不变的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her support remained constant through every difficult year.  她的支持在每个艰难年头都不曾改变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 continuous</td><td>/kənˈtɪnjuːəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>连续的，不间断的</td><td>a continuous line<br>一条连续的线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The machine has been in continuous operation for six days.  这台机器已连续运转六天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incessant</td><td>/ɪnˈsesnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无间断的</td><td>incessant noise<br>不间断的噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The incessant drumming from next door kept us awake all night.  隔壁不间断的鼓声让我们整夜无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unceasing</td><td>/ənˈsiːsɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不停的，持续的</td><td>unceasing effort<br>不懈的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unceasing questions finally uncovered the truth.  她不停的追问最终揭开了真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>interregnum</b></td><td>/ˌɪntəˈreɡnəm/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>过渡期</td><td>a brief interregnum<br>短暂的过渡期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brief interregnum followed the old king's death before his nephew arrived.  老国王去世后出现了一段短暂的政权空缺期，直到他侄子到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 breach</td><td>/briːtʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>违背</td><td>breach the contract<br>违约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company breached the contract by delivering the goods late.  这家公司延迟交货，违反了合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 break</td><td>/ˈbreɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>损坏，打破；间歇</td><td>break the vase<br>打破花瓶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cat broke the vase while chasing a moth indoors.  猫在屋里追蛾子时打破了花瓶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gap</td><td>/ˈɡæp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>缺口，隘口；差距</td><td>a gap in the hills<br>山间的隘口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A narrow gap in the ridge let the river through.  山脊上一道狭窄的缺口让河水穿过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interim</td><td>/ˈɪntərɪm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>中间过渡时期，间隔；暂时的</td><td>an interim report<br>中期报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee published an interim report while the investigation was still continuing.  委员会在调查仍在进行时发布了一份中期报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interruption</td><td>/ˌɪntəˈrʌpʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>中断，打扰</td><td>without interruption<br>不受打扰地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She worked for three hours without a single interruption.  她连续工作三小时未被打扰一次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interval</td><td>/ˈɪntərvəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>间隔，间歇</td><td>at regular intervals<br>每隔一定时间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Buses arrive at ten-minute intervals during rush hour.  高峰时段公交每隔十分钟一班。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interlude</td><td>/ˈɪntərluːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>间隙，插曲</td><td>a brief interlude<br>短暂的间隙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trip was a pleasant interlude in a very busy year.  这次旅行是忙碌一年中愉快的插曲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parenthesis</td><td>/pəˈrenθəsɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>间断</td><td>a brief parenthesis<br>短暂的间断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The war was only a brief parenthesis in the peaceful history of the town.  那场战争只是这个小镇平静历史中一段短暂的间断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 continuation</td><td>/kənˌtɪnjuˈeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>继续，延续</td><td>the continuation of talks<br>会谈的继续</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The continuation of the project depends on further funding.  项目的继续取决于后续资金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 continuity</td><td>/ˌkɑːntəˈnuːəti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>连续性，持续</td><td>ensure continuity of service<br>保证服务的连续</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The handover plan preserves continuity in patient care.  交接方案保证了病患护理的连续性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>interrogate</b></td><td>/ɪnˈterəɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>质问，审问</td><td>interrogate a suspect<br>审问嫌疑人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Detectives spent six hours interrogating the suspect about the missing money.  侦探花了六个小时审问嫌疑人关于失踪钱款的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ask</td><td>/ˈæsk/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>问，询问；请求</td><td>ask a direct question<br>问一个直接的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reporter asked the senator about the missing funds.  记者就失踪的资金询问参议员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grill</td><td>/ˈɡrɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>盘问，追问；烤</td><td>grill a witness<br>盘问证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lawyers grilled the executive for nearly four hours.  律师们盘问这位高管近四小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inquire</td><td>/ɪnˈkwaɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>询问，查问</td><td>inquire about the price<br>询问价格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She inquired politely about the train's departure time.  她礼貌地询问列车发车时间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 milk</td><td>/mɪlk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>榨取（财富、信息等）</td><td>milk a source for information<br>从线人处榨取信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters tried to milk the official for details he was not allowed to share.  记者试图从这位官员那里榨取他无权透露的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 question</td><td>/ˈkwestʃən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>询问，质疑；问题</td><td>question a witness<br>询问证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police questioned three men about the late-night break-in.  警方就深夜入室盗窃询问了三名男子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 answer</td><td>/ˈænsər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>回答，答复</td><td>answer the question honestly<br>诚实回答问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered every question without hesitation or notes.  他毫不犹豫、不看笔记地回答了每个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reply</td><td>/rɪˈplaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>回答，答复</td><td>reply to a letter<br>回一封信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She replied to my message within a few minutes.  她几分钟内就回复了我的消息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 respond</td><td>/rɪˈspɑːnd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>回应，作出反应</td><td>respond to criticism<br>回应批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company responded quickly to customer complaints online.  公司迅速回应了网上的客户投诉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>intimate</b></td><td>/ˈɪntəmeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>暗示，透露；亲密的</td><td>intimate one's intentions<br>透露自己的意图</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister intimated that new rules would appear next month.  部长暗示下月将出台新规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 familiar</td><td>/fəˈmɪljər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟悉的，通晓的</td><td>familiar with the subject<br>熟悉这门学科</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her voice sounded strangely familiar over the phone.  她的声音在电话里听起来奇怪地熟悉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 close</td><td>/kloʊz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>关闭；结束</td><td>close the meeting<br>结束会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They closed the discussion before anyone could object.  他们在有人反对之前结束了讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confidential</td><td>/ˌkɑːnfəˈdenʃəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>机密的，私密的</td><td>confidential information<br>机密信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These medical records are strictly confidential and cannot be shared.  这些病历严格保密，不得外传。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allude</td><td>/əˈluːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>间接提到，暗指</td><td>allude to a problem<br>间接提到某个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She alluded to the scandal without ever mentioning any names.  她间接提到了那桩丑闻，却从未点名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 connote</td><td>/kəˈnoʊt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>暗示，隐含（意味）</td><td>connote a hidden meaning<br>隐含言外之意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For many readers the word cheap connotes poor quality.  对许多读者来说 cheap 一词隐含质量差的意味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hint</td><td>/ˈhɪnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>暗示，提示</td><td>drop a hint<br>透露一点暗示</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave me a subtle hint about the surprise party.  她微妙地暗示了那个惊喜派对。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imply</td><td>/ɪmˈplaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>暗示，含有…意思</td><td>imply a criticism<br>暗含批评之意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Silence at such a moment may imply consent rather than doubt.  此刻的沉默可能意味着同意而非怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indicate</td><td>/ˈɪndəˌkeɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>表明，示意</td><td>indicate a preference<br>表明偏好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The survey results indicate a growing distrust of large banks.  调查结果表明人们对大银行的不信任在加深。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infer</td><td>/ɪnˈfɜːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>推断，推论</td><td>infer from the evidence<br>从证据中推断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From his tone we inferred that the negotiations had failed.  从他的语气我们推断谈判已经失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suggest</td><td>/səˈdʒest/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>暗示；建议</td><td>suggest an alternative<br>提出替代方案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her hesitation suggested that she knew more than she admitted.  她的犹豫暗示她知道的比承认的更多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 distant</td><td>/ˈdɪstənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷淡的，疏远的；遥远的</td><td>a distant manner<br>冷淡的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was polite but distant throughout the whole dinner.  整个晚餐期间她客气却冷淡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 remote</td><td>/rɪˈmoʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷淡的，疏离的；遥远的</td><td>a remote expression<br>疏离的神情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His manner remained remote even among close family members.  即使在近亲之间他也显得疏离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 articulate</td><td>/ɑːrˈtɪkjuleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>清晰地表达；表达清晰的</td><td>articulate her concerns<br>清晰表达她的顾虑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She managed to articulate her concerns clearly at the board meeting.  她在董事会上清晰地表达了自己的顾虑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>intimidate</b></td><td>/ɪnˈtɪmɪdeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>威吓</td><td>intimidate the witness<br>威吓证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defendant's friends tried to intimidate the witness before the trial began.  被告的朋友试图在审判开始前威吓证人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 browbeat</td><td>/ˈbraʊbiːt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恐吓</td><td>browbeat the witness<br>恐吓证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer tried to browbeat the witness into changing her story.  律师试图恐吓证人改口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bully</td><td>/ˈbʊli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>欺凌弱小者，恶霸</td><td>stand up to a bully<br>勇敢面对恶霸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new teacher quickly stopped the bully who terrorized younger children.  新老师很快制止了那个欺负小同学的恶霸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coerce</td><td>/koʊˈɜːrs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（以武力）强制</td><td>coerce him into silence<br>强迫他保持沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soldiers coerced the villagers into handing over their grain.  士兵强迫村民交出粮食。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cow</td><td>/kaʊ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恐吓，威胁</td><td>cow them into obedience<br>威吓他们服从</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sergeant's shouting failed to cow the new recruits into silence.  中士的吼叫没能把新兵威吓得沉默下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frighten</td><td>/ˈfraɪtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使惊恐，使害怕</td><td>frighten away the birds<br>把鸟惊走</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A loud noise frightened the horses into the open field.  一声巨响把马惊到了空旷的田里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hector</td><td>/ˈhektər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>威吓，欺凌恫吓</td><td>hector the staff<br>恫吓下属</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach hectored his players throughout the second half.  整个下半场教练都在对球员大声恫吓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 terrify</td><td>/ˈterəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恐惧，使惊吓</td><td>terrify the children<br>使孩子们害怕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud thunder terrifies our dog every single summer night.  响雷每个夏夜都让我们的狗害怕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 blandish</del></td><td>/ˈblændɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>奉承，讨好，哄诱</td><td>blandish the boss<br>讨好奉承老板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He blandished the officials until they signed the permit.  他一路奉承官员，直到他们签下许可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cajole</td><td>/kəˈdʒoʊl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>哄骗</td><td>cajole him into agreeing<br>哄他答应下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She cajoled her brother into driving her to the airport.  她哄着哥哥开车送她去机场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 coax</td><td>/koʊks/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>哄骗</td><td>coax him into agreeing<br>哄他答应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She coaxed the frightened kitten out from under the porch.  她把受惊的小猫从门廊下哄了出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>intoxicant</b></td><td>/ɪnˈtɑːksɪkənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>使人陶醉的东西（尤指酒精饮料）</td><td>a powerful intoxicant<br>强效的醉人之物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wine was the only intoxicant served at the wedding banquet.  婚宴上供应的唯一致醉饮品是葡萄酒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alcohol</td><td>/ˈælkəˌhɑːl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>酒精，含酒精饮料</td><td>avoid alcohol completely<br>完全戒酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave up alcohol after his doctor's stern warning.  医生严厉警告后他戒了酒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 liquor</td><td>/ˈlɪkər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>烈酒，酒</td><td>a bottle of liquor<br>一瓶烈酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The store cannot sell liquor before noon on Sundays.  这家店周日中午前不得售卖烈酒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stimulant</td><td>/ˈstɪmjələnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>兴奋剂，刺激物</td><td>a mild stimulant<br>温和的兴奋剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Coffee acts as a stimulant for most healthy adults.  对大多数健康成年人来说咖啡起兴奋剂作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refresher</td><td>/rɪˈfreʃər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>提神饮料；复习课程</td><td>a refresher course<br>进修复习课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Iced tea made a welcome refresher after the long hike.  长途徒步后冰茶成了令人愉快的提神饮料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>intransigent</b></td><td>/ɪnˈtrænzɪdʒənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不妥协的，固执的</td><td>an intransigent attitude<br>不妥协的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides remained intransigent, so the negotiations collapsed.  双方都毫不妥协，谈判因此破裂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adamant</td><td>/ˈædəmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，不可动摇的</td><td>adamant about the rules<br>对规则毫不通融</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach was adamant that no player would skip practice.  教练坚决要求没有球员可以缺席训练。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 headstrong</td><td>/ˈhedˌstrɔːŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>任性的，固执的</td><td>a headstrong child<br>任性的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The headstrong young man ignored every warning from his parents.  这个任性的年轻人对父母的每句劝告都置之不理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intractable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtræktəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难对付的，棘手的</td><td>an intractable problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty remains an intractable problem in many rural regions.  贫困在许多农村地区仍是棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstinate</td><td>/ˈɑːbstɪnət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的</td><td>an obstinate refusal<br>固执的拒绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her obstinate refusal to compromise delayed the agreement by several months.  她固执地拒绝妥协，使协议推迟了几个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 pertinacious</del></td><td>/ˌpɜːrtnˈeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽固的，固执地坚持的</td><td>pertinacious in her demands<br>固执地坚持要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pertinacious reporter kept calling until she got an answer.  这位执着的记者不停打电话，直到得到答复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stubborn</td><td>/ˈstʌbərn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，顽固的</td><td>as stubborn as a mule<br>像骡子一样固执</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My grandfather was too stubborn to see a doctor.  我祖父太固执，不肯去看医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncompromising</td><td>/ʌnˈkɑːmprəmaɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不妥协的，坚定的</td><td>an uncompromising stance<br>毫不妥协的立场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took an uncompromising stand against corruption in the department.  她对部门内的腐败采取了毫不妥协的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unyielding</td><td>/ʌnˈjiːldɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不屈的，不让步的</td><td>unyielding opposition<br>毫不让步的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union remained unyielding on the question of wages.  工会在工资问题上依然毫不让步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pliable</td><td>/ˈplaɪəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的；易受影响的，温顺的</td><td>pliable young minds<br>易受影响的年轻心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young minds are pliable and easily shaped by the adults around them.  年轻的心智易受影响，很容易被身边的成年人塑造。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>intrepid</b></td><td>/ɪnˈtrepɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大胆的</td><td>an intrepid explorer<br>无畏的探险家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The intrepid explorer crossed the frozen strait alone in the middle of winter.  这位大胆的探险家在隆冬时节独自穿越了结冰的海峡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 audacious</td><td>/ɔːˈdeɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大胆的，（但往往）愚勇的；令人吃惊的</td><td>an audacious plan<br>大胆的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general approved an audacious plan to cross the river at night.  将军批准了一个夜间渡河的大胆计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brave</td><td>/ˈbreɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>勇敢的</td><td>a brave decision<br>勇敢的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was brave of her to speak against the new policy.  她敢于反对新政策，很勇敢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bold</td><td>/ˈboʊld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大胆的，勇敢的；醒目的</td><td>a bold move<br>大胆的举动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was a bold decision to leave a secure job.  辞去稳定的工作是个大胆的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 courageous</td><td>/kəˈreɪdʒəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>勇敢的，有胆量的</td><td>a courageous decision<br>勇敢的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was courageous of him to speak out against the policy.  他公开反对这项政策，很有勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dauntless</td><td>/ˈdɔntləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无畏的，勇敢的</td><td>dauntless courage<br>无畏的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dauntless nurse worked through shelling to reach the wounded.  这位无畏的护士在炮击中赶去救伤员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fearless</td><td>/ˈfɪrləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无畏的，大胆的</td><td>a fearless reporter<br>无畏的记者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was fearless in questioning the most powerful men present.  她敢于质问在场最有权势的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gallant</td><td>/ˈɡælənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>英勇的</td><td>a gallant defense<br>英勇的防守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gallant paratroopers jumped from the plane without a moment's hesitation.  英勇的伞兵们毫不犹豫地跳出了飞机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 valiant</td><td>/ˈvæliənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>勇敢的，英勇的</td><td>a valiant effort<br>英勇的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A valiant firefighter carried two children out of the burning building.  一位英勇的消防员把两个孩子抱出了燃烧的大楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 valorous</td><td>/ˈvælərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>勇猛的，英勇的</td><td>a valorous knight<br>英勇的骑士</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Songs still honor the valorous soldiers who held the bridge.  歌曲仍在颂扬守住那座桥的英勇士兵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 apprehensive</td><td>/ˌæprɪˈhensɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>知晓的；忧虑不安的</td><td>apprehensive of the danger<br>知晓其中的危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was fully apprehensive of the dangers before she signed anything.  在签任何文件之前，她已充分知晓其中的危险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>intrigue</b></td><td>/ˈɪntriːɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>阴谋；激起…的兴趣</td><td>political intrigue<br>政治阴谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Court records reveal decades of political intrigue behind the sudden fall of the minister.  法庭记录揭示了这位大臣突然倒台背后数十年的政治阴谋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conspiracy</td><td>/kənˈspɪrəsi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>阴谋，共谋</td><td>a conspiracy to defraud<br>诈骗的共谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two officials were charged with conspiracy to hide the evidence.  两名官员被控共谋隐藏证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 design</td><td>/dɪˈzaɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>图谋，意图；设计</td><td>an evil design<br>邪恶的图谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He denied having any design against his former partner.  他否认对前合伙人有任何图谋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 machination</td><td>/ˌmæʃɪˈneɪʃn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>诡计</td><td>political machinations<br>政治阴谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careful readers can trace the machinations that brought down the old regime.  细心的读者能追溯到使旧政权垮台的种种诡计。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scheme</td><td>/ˈskiːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>阴谋，计划</td><td>a scheme to defraud investors<br>诈骗投资者的阴谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They devised a scheme to avoid paying any taxes.  他们设计了一个逃税的计谋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appeal</td><td>/əˈpiːl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>上诉；恳求，呼吁</td><td>file an appeal<br>提出上诉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lawyers will file an appeal against the verdict next week.  她的律师下周将针对判决提出上诉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attract</td><td>/əˈtrækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>吸引，引起</td><td>attract attention<br>吸引注意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exhibition attracted thousands of visitors in its first week.  展览首周吸引了数千名参观者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enthrall</td><td>/ɪnˈθrɔːl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（像用魔咒般）吸引</td><td>enthrall the entire audience<br>吸引住全场观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magician enthralled the entire audience with a single deck of cards.  魔术师仅用一副牌就吸引住了全场观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entice</td><td>/ɪnˈtaɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱使，引诱</td><td>entice customers with discounts<br>用折扣吸引顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Free samples were used to entice shoppers into the new store.  免费样品被用来引诱购物者进入新店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excite</td><td>/ɪkˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，引发；使兴奋</td><td>excite public interest<br>激起公众兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech excited strong resentment among the workers.  他的演讲激起了工人们的强烈不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fascinate</td><td>/ˈfæsɪneɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强烈吸引，使入迷</td><td>fascinate young readers<br>使小读者着迷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stories about strange deep-sea creatures fascinate young readers all over the world.  关于奇异深海生物的故事让全世界的小读者着迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interest</td><td>/ˈɪntrəst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>兴趣，关注；利息</td><td>show interest in music<br>对音乐表现出兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She lost interest in the project after only a month.  仅一个月后她就对这个项目失去了兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 occupy</td><td>/ˈɑːkjəˌpaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>占据，占用；占领</td><td>occupy the whole floor<br>占据整层楼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Troops occupied the town for six months before withdrawing quietly.  部队占领该镇六个月后悄然撤离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ennui</td><td>/ɑːnˈwiː/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>倦怠；缺乏兴趣</td><td>a deep sense of ennui<br>深深的倦怠感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A deep sense of ennui settled over the students during the long summer.  漫长的夏天里，一种深深的倦怠感笼罩着学生们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pall</td><td>/pɔːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>令人厌倦，失去吸引力</td><td>begin to pall<br>开始令人生厌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novelty of city life began to pall after his first lonely winter.  在孤独的第一个冬天过后，城市生活的新鲜感开始让他生厌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 2

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>intrinsic</b></td><td>/ɪnˈtrɪnsɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固有的，内在的</td><td>intrinsic value<br>内在价值</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting has an intrinsic beauty independent of its price.  这幅画有着与价格无关的内在美。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 congenial</td><td>/kənˈdʒiːniəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>和善的，友好的</td><td>a congenial host<br>一位和善的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our congenial host made every guest feel completely at home.  我们和善的主人让每位客人都感到自在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 constitutional</td><td>/ˌkɑːnstəˈtuːʃənəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>体质上的，内在固有的</td><td>a constitutional weakness<br>体质上的虚弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His shyness seems constitutional rather than a habit he learned.  他的害羞似乎是天性使然，而非后天习得的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immanent</td><td>/ˈɪmənənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>内在的</td><td>immanent in nature<br>内在于自然之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He argued that order is immanent in nature rather than imposed on it.  他认为秩序内在于自然，而非外加于自然。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inborn</td><td>/ˈɪnˌbɔːrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>天生的，先天的</td><td>an inborn talent<br>天生的才能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She seems to have an inborn sense of rhythm.  她似乎天生就有节奏感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inherent</td><td>/ɪnˈhɪrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>内在的，本质的</td><td>an inherent risk<br>固有的风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is an inherent danger in climbing without a rope.  无绳攀岩存在内在的危险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 innate</td><td>/ɪˈneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>天生的，固有的</td><td>an innate ability<br>天生的能力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children have an innate curiosity about the world.  儿童对世界有一种天生的好奇心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 native</td><td>/ˈneɪtɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>本地的，原产的；本地人</td><td>native to southern China<br>原产于华南</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This bamboo is native to the wet valleys of the south.  这种竹子原产于南方潮湿的山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 natural</td><td>/ˈnætʃərəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自然的，正常的</td><td>a natural reaction<br>自然的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is natural to feel nervous before a big exam.  大考前紧张是很自然的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adventitious</td><td>/ˌædvenˈtɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>外来的，后天的，非内在的</td><td>an adventitious circumstance<br>偶然的外部情况</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her success was not adventitious but the result of years of work.  她的成功不是偶然外来的，而是多年努力的结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extraneous</td><td>/ɪkˈstreɪniəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无关的，不重要的</td><td>extraneous information<br>无关的信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please delete all extraneous details from the summary.  请把摘要中所有无关的细节删掉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extrinsic</td><td>/eksˈtrɪnsɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>外在的，非固有的</td><td>extrinsic motivation<br>外在的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Money is an extrinsic reward that rarely sustains real interest.  金钱是一种外在奖励，很少能维持真正的兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>intuitive</b></td><td>/ɪnˈtuːɪtɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>直觉的</td><td>an intuitive understanding<br>直觉上的理解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good nurses often have an intuitive sense of when a patient is worsening.  优秀的护士常常凭直觉感知病人何时病情恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instinctive</td><td>/ɪnˈstɪŋktɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>本能的，直觉的</td><td>an instinctive reaction<br>本能的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her instinctive distrust of the offer proved entirely correct.  她对这份提议本能的不信任完全正确。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acquired</td><td>/əˈkwaɪərd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>后天获得的，习得的</td><td>an acquired taste<br>后天养成的口味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fluency in Latin is entirely an acquired skill.  拉丁语流利完全是后天习得的技能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inundate</b></td><td>/ˈɪnʌndeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>淹没</td><td>inundated with requests<br>被请求淹没</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers inundated the newspaper with letters after the controversial editorial appeared.  那篇有争议的社论刊出后，读者的来信淹没了报社。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avalanche</td><td>/ˈævəˌlæntʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>雪崩；大量涌来</td><td>an avalanche of letters<br>如雪崩般的信件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The announcement triggered an avalanche of angry emails.  这则公告引发了如雪崩般的愤怒邮件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deluge</td><td>/ˈdeljuːdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大暴雨；大量</td><td>a deluge of complaints<br>大量投诉涌来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The announcement brought a deluge of angry letters to the editor.  这项公告引来大量愤怒的读者来信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drown</td><td>/ˈdraʊn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>溺死；淹没</td><td>drown out the noise<br>把噪音压过去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud applause drowned out the speaker's closing words completely.  响亮的掌声完全淹没了演讲者的结束语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 engulf</td><td>/ɪnˈɡʌlf/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>吞没，包围</td><td>engulfed in flames<br>被火焰吞没</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fire quickly engulfed the abandoned warehouse by the river.  大火迅速吞没了河边的废弃仓库。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overflow</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈfloʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>溢出，泛滥</td><td>overflow the river banks<br>河水漫过堤岸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Melting snow made the river overflow its banks last spring.  去年春天融雪使河水漫过堤岸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overwhelm</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈwelm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>淹没</td><td>overwhelmed by work<br>被工作压得喘不过气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That spring the whole valley was overwhelmed by floods from melting snow.  那年春天，整个山谷被融雪形成的洪水淹没。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 submerge</td><td>/səbˈmɜːrdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使淹没</td><td>submerge the whole valley<br>淹没整个山谷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new dam will submerge the whole valley and three old villages.  新水坝将淹没整个山谷和三个古村。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 drain</td><td>/ˈdreɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>耗尽，使枯竭</td><td>drain the country's resources<br>耗尽国家资源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long war drained the country's resources and its morale.  漫长的战争耗尽了国家的资源和士气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inure</b></td><td>/ɪˈnjʊr/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使习惯接受不好的东西</td><td>inured to hardship<br>习惯了艰苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years at sea had inured the fishermen to cold, wet, and hunger.  多年的海上生活使渔民们习惯了寒冷、潮湿和饥饿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accustom</td><td>/əˈkʌstəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使习惯</td><td>accustom oneself to cold<br>使自己适应寒冷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He had to accustom himself to the cold Boston winters very quickly.  他必须很快让自己习惯波士顿寒冷的冬天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 familiarize</td><td>/fəˈmɪliəraɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使熟悉，使了解</td><td>familiarize yourself with the rules<br>让自己熟悉规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New staff must familiarize themselves with safety procedures.  新员工必须熟悉安全流程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 habituate</td><td>/həˈbɪtʃuˌeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使习惯于</td><td>habituate oneself to cold<br>使自己习惯寒冷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Divers slowly habituate themselves to the pressure of deep water.  潜水员慢慢让自己习惯深水的压力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>invective</b></td><td>/ɪnˈvektɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>谩骂，痛骂；侮辱性的</td><td>a stream of invective<br>一连串的谩骂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The politician's invective attacks on his rival shocked even his own supporters.  这位政客对对手的侮辱性攻击甚至震惊了他自己的支持者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abusive</td><td>/əˈbjuːsɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>辱骂的，虐待的</td><td>abusive language<br>辱骂性的语言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The referee ejected the player for abusive language.  裁判因辱骂性言语将该球员罚出场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 opprobrious</del></td><td>/əˈproʊbriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>辱骂的；臭名昭著的，可耻的</td><td>opprobrious remarks<br>辱骂性的言辞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlet was filled with opprobrious attacks on the mayor.  那本小册子充满了对市长的辱骂性攻击。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 scurrile</del></td><td>/ˈskɜːrəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗俗下流的，谩骂的</td><td>scurrile jests<br>下流的笑话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics attacked the pamphlet for its scurrile tone.  批评者攻击这本小册子语调粗俗下流。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scurrilous</td><td>/ˈskɜːrələs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>恶语中伤的，下流的</td><td>scurrilous accusations<br>下流的指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlet contained scurrilous attacks on every member of the council.  那本小册子对议会每位成员都有下流的攻击。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 truculent</td><td>/ˈtrʌkjələnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好战的，好斗的；粗野的</td><td>a truculent response<br>咄咄逼人的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The truculent teenager argued with every teacher who tried to correct him.  这个好斗的少年跟每位试图纠正他的老师都吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vitriolic</td><td>/ˌvɪtriˈɑːlɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（言辞）刻薄的</td><td>a vitriolic attack<br>尖刻的攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The review was so vitriolic that the author stopped writing.  那篇评论如此刻薄，作者因此停笔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vituperative</td><td>/ˌvaɪˈtuːpərətɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谩骂的，斥责的</td><td>a vituperative attack<br>谩骂式的攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His vituperative letter to the editor was never printed.  他写给编辑的那封谩骂信从未刊出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 adulatory</del></td><td>/ˈædʒələˌtɔri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谄媚的，过分吹捧的</td><td>an adulatory review<br>吹捧的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine ran an adulatory profile of the young director.  杂志刊登了一篇吹捧那位年轻导演的报道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flattery</td><td>/ˈflætəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>奉承，谄媚</td><td>shameless flattery<br>无耻的奉承</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He won the manager's favor through steady flattery rather than work.  他靠不断奉承而非工作赢得了经理的青睐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>inveigh</b></del></td><td>/ɪnˈveɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>猛烈抨击，痛斥</td><td>inveigh against the new tax<br>激烈抗议新税</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Farmers gathered downtown to inveigh against the new fuel tax.  农民们聚集在市中心痛斥新的燃油税。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gripe</td><td>/ɡraɪp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>抱怨</td><td>gripe about the food<br>抱怨饭菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The passengers griped loudly about the delay for nearly a whole hour.  乘客们大声抱怨延误，抱怨了将近一个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grouse</td><td>/ˈɡraʊs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>抱怨，发牢骚</td><td>grouse about long hours<br>抱怨工时太长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crew groused about long hours during the harvest season.  收割季里工人们抱怨工时太长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 object</td><td>/əbˈdʒekt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>反对，抗议</td><td>object to the proposal<br>反对该提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several neighbors objected to the new parking rules.  几位邻居反对新的停车规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 protest</td><td>/ˈproʊˌtest/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>抗议，反对</td><td>register a formal protest<br>提出正式抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ambassador registered a formal protest with the foreign ministry.  大使向外交部提出正式抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remonstrate</td><td>/rɪˈmɑːnstreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>抗议，规劝</td><td>remonstrate with the manager<br>向经理提出抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remonstrated with the driver about his reckless speed.  她就司机的危险车速向他抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 repine</del></td><td>/rɪˈpaɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>抱怨，表达不满；渴望</td><td>repine at one's fate<br>抱怨自己的命运</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rather than repine at his losses, he quietly started the business again.  他没有抱怨自己的损失，而是默默重新开始了生意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 support</td><td>/səˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，支撑</td><td>support a proposal<br>支持一项提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most members voted to support the new proposal.  大多数成员投票支持这项新提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inveigle</b></td><td>/ɪnˈveɪɡl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱骗；诱使；哄骗</td><td>inveigle someone into agreeing<br>诱骗某人同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She inveigled her brother into lending her the car for the whole weekend.  她哄骗哥哥把车借给她用整个周末。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allure</td><td>/əˈlʊr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>吸引，诱惑</td><td>the allure of fame<br>名声的诱惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap rents allure young artists to this run-down district.  低廉的租金吸引年轻艺术家来到这个破旧街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bait</td><td>/beɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>故意惹恼，戏弄；引诱</td><td>bait him about his accent<br>拿他的口音戏弄他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The older boys baited him about his accent until he cried.  大孩子们拿他的口音戏弄他，直到他哭了出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decoy</td><td>/ˈdiːkɔɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>诱饵，圈套；诱骗</td><td>act as a decoy<br>充当诱饵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wooden duck served as a decoy to attract real birds.  木鸭子被用作诱饵来吸引真鸟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entice</td><td>/ɪnˈtaɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱使，引诱</td><td>entice customers with discounts<br>用折扣吸引顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Free samples were used to entice shoppers into the new store.  免费样品被用来引诱购物者进入新店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entrap</td><td>/ɪnˈtræp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱骗</td><td>entrap an unwary buyer<br>诱骗不留神的买主</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The con artist entrapped an unwary buyer with a forged certificate.  骗子用一张伪造证书诱骗了一位不留神的买主。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 seduce</td><td>/sɪˈduːs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱使（背离正道），引诱</td><td>seduce someone into crime<br>引诱某人犯罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 High salaries seduced many teachers away from the classroom.  高薪诱使许多教师离开了课堂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tempt</td><td>/ˈtempt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱惑，引诱</td><td>tempt fate<br>冒险行事，玩火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smell of fresh bread tempted us into the little bakery.  新鲜面包的香味把我们诱进了那家小面包店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 demand</td><td>/dɪˈmænd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>强烈要求；需求</td><td>demand an apology<br>要求道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The angry customers demanded an apology from the store manager.  愤怒的顾客要求店长道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inventory</b></td><td>/ˈɪnvəntɔːri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>详细清单，清点</td><td>take inventory of skills<br>盘点自身技能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report offers a valuable inventory of everything known about this virus.  这份报告对目前关于这种病毒的全部认识做了有价值的清点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abstract</td><td>/æbˈstrækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>做总结，概括；使分心</td><td>abstract a long report<br>概括一份长报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She abstracted the key findings into a single page.  她把主要发现概括成一页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brief</td><td>/ˈbriːf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简短的，简洁的</td><td>a brief summary<br>简短的总结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a brief account of the accident to the police.  他向警方简短叙述了事故经过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 epitome</td><td>/ɪˈpɪtəmi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>缩影，典型；摘要</td><td>the epitome of elegance<br>优雅的典范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That small hotel is the epitome of quiet good taste.  那家小旅馆是安静品味的典范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outline</td><td>/ˈaʊtˌlaɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>概要，大纲；概述</td><td>a rough outline<br>粗略的大纲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sketched an outline of the project in ten minutes.  他十分钟内勾出了项目的大纲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 résumé</td><td>/ˈrezəmeɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>摘要；简历</td><td>send in a résumé<br>投递一份简历</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She updated her résumé before applying for the position.  她申请该职位前更新了简历。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 summarization</td><td>/ˌsʌmərəˈzeɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>概括，总结</td><td>automatic text summarization<br>自动文本摘要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report ends with a brief summarization of the findings.  报告以对研究发现的简短概括结尾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 synopsis</td><td>/sɪˈnɑːpsɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>摘要，概要</td><td>a brief synopsis<br>简短的概要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please send a brief synopsis of your novel with the application.  请随申请寄来你小说的简短概要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inveterate</b></td><td>/ɪnˈvetərət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>根深蒂固的</td><td>an inveterate gambler<br>积习难改的赌徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An inveterate gambler, he lost the family savings within a single year.  他是个积习难改的赌徒，一年之内就输光了家中积蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chronic</td><td>/ˈkrɑːnɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>经常发生的，复发的</td><td>chronic illness<br>慢性疾病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Chronic shortages of water have plagued the region for decades.  长期缺水困扰该地区数十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entrenched</td><td>/enˈtrentʃt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>根深蒂固的</td><td>deeply entrenched habits<br>根深蒂固的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Corruption was entrenched at every level of the ministry.  腐败在该部门各个层级根深蒂固。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ingrained</td><td>/ɪnˈɡreɪnd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>本质的，根深蒂固的</td><td>deeply ingrained habits<br>根深蒂固的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Habits ingrained since childhood are extremely difficult to change in later life.  童年就已根深蒂固的习惯到了晚年极难改变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rooted</td><td>/ˈruːtəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>根深蒂固的，扎根的</td><td>rooted in tradition<br>植根于传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her fear of water was rooted in a childhood accident.  她对水的恐惧源于童年一次事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 settled</td><td>/ˈsetəld/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固定的，安定的</td><td>a settled routine<br>固定的作息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After years of moving they finally led a settled life.  多年漂泊后他们终于过上安定的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hard-cored</td><td>/ˈhɑːrd ˈkɔːrd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽固的，死硬的</td><td>hard-cored supporters<br>死忠的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only hard-cored fans stayed until the very last song.  只有死忠粉丝留到最后一首歌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adventitious</td><td>/ˌædvenˈtɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>外来的，后天的，非内在的</td><td>an adventitious circumstance<br>偶然的外部情况</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her success was not adventitious but the result of years of work.  她的成功不是偶然外来的，而是多年努力的结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>invidious</b></td><td>/ɪnˈvɪdiəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>惹人反感的；羡慕嫉妒恨的</td><td>an invidious comparison<br>招人反感的比较</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ranking the students publicly created invidious comparisons that poisoned the whole class.  公开给学生排名制造了招人反感的比较，毒害了整个班级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abhorrent</td><td>/æbˈhɔːrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌恶的</td><td>abhorrent to most people<br>令多数人厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The practice is abhorrent to anyone with basic decency.  这种做法令任何有基本良知的人厌恶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detestable</td><td>/dɪˈtɛstəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可憎的，令人厌恶的</td><td>a detestable habit<br>令人厌恶的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He found the general's casual cruelty absolutely detestable.  他觉得那位将军随意的残暴极其可憎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obnoxious</td><td>/əbˈnɑːkʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极讨厌的，令人反感的</td><td>an obnoxious smell<br>令人恶心的气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His obnoxious jokes ruined the whole dinner party.  他讨人厌的玩笑毁了整场晚宴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 odious</td><td>/ˈoʊdiəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可憎的，令人厌恶的</td><td>an odious task<br>讨厌的差事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Comparing the two writers seems both odious and pointless.  比较这两位作家显得可憎又无意义。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repugnant</td><td>/rɪˈpʌɡnənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌恶的</td><td>morally repugnant behavior<br>道德上可憎的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most readers found the cruelty of that character morally repugnant and unforgivable.  多数读者觉得那个角色的残忍在道德上令人厌恶，不可宽恕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repellent</td><td>/rɪˈpelənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>令人厌恶的；驱虫剂</td><td>a repellent odor<br>令人厌恶的气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I found his arrogant remarks utterly repellent and left early.  我觉得他傲慢的言论令人极其厌恶，于是提前离开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 covetous</td><td>/ˈkʌvətəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪婪的，渴求财富的</td><td>covetous of his wealth<br>贪求他的财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He cast a covetous eye on his neighbor's newly bought tractor.  他贪婪地盯着邻居新买的拖拉机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 envious</td><td>/ˈenviəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>羡慕的，嫉妒的</td><td>envious of her success<br>嫉妒她的成功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was openly envious of his cousin's new car.  他公开嫉妒表弟的新车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 Jaundiced</td><td>/ˈdʒɔːndɪst/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有偏见的，怀恨的</td><td>a jaundiced view<br>带偏见的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of disappointment gave him a jaundiced view of politics.  多年失望让他对政治抱有偏见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jealous</td><td>/ˈdʒeləs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>嫉妒的</td><td>jealous of a rival<br>嫉妒对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew jealous when he praised another student's essay.  他称赞另一名学生的文章时，她心生嫉妒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resentful</td><td>/rɪˈzentfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>愤恨的，不满的</td><td>resentful of criticism<br>对批评心怀不满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew resentful when her contributions were repeatedly ignored.  当她的付出被一再忽视时，她开始心生怨恨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 green-eyed</td><td>/ˈɡriːn aɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>嫉妒的</td><td>the green-eyed monster<br>嫉妒这头怪物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He became green-eyed the moment his friend won the prize.  朋友获奖那一刻他就妒火中烧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agreeable</td><td>/əˈɡriːəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人愉悦的；相一致的</td><td>an agreeable companion<br>一个讨人喜欢的同伴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She found him an agreeable companion on the long train journey.  她觉得他是长途火车旅程中一位讨人喜欢的同伴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gratifying</td><td>/ˈɡrætəˌfaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人满意的，令人欣慰的</td><td>a gratifying result<br>令人满意的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was gratifying to see her hard work finally rewarded.  看到她的努力终获回报令人欣慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pleasant</td><td>/ˈplezənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人愉快的；和善的</td><td>a pleasant surprise<br>令人愉快的惊喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We spent a pleasant afternoon walking along the quiet river.  我们沿着静静的河边散步，度过了愉快的下午。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 3

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>invigorate</b></td><td>/ɪnˈvɪɡəreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使精神，使强壮，鼓舞激励</td><td>invigorate the local economy<br>振兴当地经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk in cold air always invigorates my tired mind.  在冷空气中短暂散步总能让我疲惫的头脑重新振作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 animate</td><td>/ˈænəmeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使有生气，激励；有活力的</td><td>animate the discussion<br>让讨论活跃起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A few well-chosen questions can animate an otherwise dull discussion.  几个精心挑选的问题就能让本来沉闷的讨论活跃起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 energize</td><td>/ˈenərdʒaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使充满活力，激励</td><td>energize the whole team<br>使整个团队振奋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new coach energized a team that had lost hope.  新教练让失去希望的球队重新振奋起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fortify</td><td>/ˈfɔːrtɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加固；增强，强化</td><td>fortify a city wall<br>加固城墙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The milk is fortified with extra vitamin D.  这种牛奶额外强化添加了维生素D。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reinforce</td><td>/ˌriːɪnˈfɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加强，强化</td><td>reinforce the message<br>强化这一信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repeated praise reinforced the child's interest in reading.  反复的表扬强化了孩子对阅读的兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strengthen</td><td>/ˈstreŋθən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>加强，增强</td><td>strengthen the economy<br>增强经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise will strengthen your heart and lungs.  规律锻炼会增强你的心肺功能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vitalize</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赋予活力，激励</td><td>vitalize the local economy<br>使当地经济充满活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New investment vitalized the sleepy town within just two years.  新投资在两年内使这个沉寂的小镇焕发活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dampen</td><td>/ˈdæmpən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，压抑（感情、精力等），泼冷水</td><td>dampen his enthusiasm<br>打击他的热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Losing the first game did nothing to dampen the team's spirits.  首场失利丝毫没有打击球队的士气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deaden</td><td>/ˈdedən/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使减弱，使麻木</td><td>deaden the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick carpets deaden the sound of footsteps upstairs.  厚地毯减弱了楼上的脚步声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debilitate</td><td>/dɪˈbɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>debilitate the immune system<br>使免疫系统衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The virus gradually debilitates the immune system of healthy adults.  这种病毒会逐渐削弱健康成年人的免疫系统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 emaciate</del></td><td>/ɪˈmeɪʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使消瘦，使憔悴</td><td>emaciate the patient's body<br>使病人身体消瘦衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months of fever had emaciated the patient's body almost beyond recognition.  数月高烧使病人的身体消瘦衰弱得几乎认不出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sap</td><td>/sæp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>削弱，逐渐耗尽</td><td>sap his strength<br>削弱他的力量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weeks of night shifts sapped the energy of the young doctor completely.  数周的夜班彻底削弱了这位年轻医生的精力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>invincible</b></td><td>/ɪnˈvɪnsəbl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可战胜的，不可超越的</td><td>an invincible army<br>不可战胜的军队</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For a decade the team seemed invincible on its own home court.  十年间，这支球队在自己的主场上似乎不可战胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bulletproof</td><td>/ˈbʊlətˌpruːf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>防弹的；无懈可击的</td><td>a bulletproof vest<br>防弹背心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyers built a bulletproof case against the company.  律师们为起诉该公司建立了无懈可击的案子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impregnable</td><td>/ɪmˈpreɡnəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚不可摧的，无法攻克的</td><td>an impregnable fortress<br>坚不可摧的堡垒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cliffside castle was long considered impregnable by invaders.  这座悬崖上的城堡长期被入侵者视为坚不可摧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invulnerable</td><td>/ɪnˈvʌlnərəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不会受伤的，无懈可击的</td><td>invulnerable to attack<br>不受攻击伤害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No fortress is truly invulnerable to a long siege.  没有哪座堡垒真能经受长期围攻而不受损。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unbeatable</td><td>/ʌnˈbiːtəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法击败的，无可匹敌的</td><td>unbeatable prices<br>无可匹敌的价格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The champion looked unbeatable throughout the entire tournament.  整个锦标赛中冠军看来无人能敌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unconquerable</td><td>/ʌnˈkɑːŋkərəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可征服的</td><td>an unconquerable spirit<br>不可征服的精神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unconquerable will carried her through the long illness.  她不可征服的意志支撑她度过长期病痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 conquerable</del></td><td>/ˈkɑːŋkərəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可征服的，可克服的</td><td>a conquerable fear<br>可以克服的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general believed the fortress was conquerable within a week.  将军相信这座要塞一周内可以攻下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>invoke</b></td><td>/ɪnˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>援引；引起，造成</td><td>invoke widespread anger<br>引发广泛的愤怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clumsy apology only invoked more anger among the injured workers.  拙劣的道歉只是在受伤的工人中引发了更多愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enforce</td><td>/enˈfɔːrs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强制执行，实施</td><td>enforce the rules<br>强制执行规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police were sent to enforce the new parking regulations.  警察被派去强制执行新的停车规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 effect</td><td>/ɪˈfekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>影响，效果；实现</td><td>have a lasting effect<br>产生持久影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new rule had little effect on daily traffic.  新规定对日常交通几乎没有影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 execute</td><td>/ˈeksɪˌkjuːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>执行，实施</td><td>execute the plan<br>执行计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team executed the rescue plan with remarkable precision.  团队极为精确地执行了救援计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 implement</td><td>/ˈɪmplɪment/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>执行，实施</td><td>implement the policy<br>执行政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city will implement the new recycling policy in early spring.  市政府将在初春执行新的回收政策。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perform</td><td>/pərˈfɔːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>表演；执行，履行</td><td>perform on stage<br>在舞台上表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist will perform on stage for the first time.  这位年轻钢琴家将首次登台表演。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beget</td><td>/bɪˈɡet/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>引起，产生；生育</td><td>violence begets violence<br>暴力引发暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careless words often beget quarrels that last for years.  轻率的言语常引发持续多年的争吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bring</td><td>/ˈbrɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>带来，引起</td><td>bring about change<br>带来改变</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new law brought about a sharp drop in traffic deaths.  新法律使交通死亡人数大幅下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 catalyze</td><td>/ˈkætəlaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>成为…的导火索，导致</td><td>catalyze reform<br>推动改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawsuit catalyzed a wave of reform across the industry.  这场诉讼引发了整个行业的改革浪潮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cause</td><td>/ˈkɔːz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>原因；导致，引起</td><td>the root cause<br>根本原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain caused the small stream to flood the road.  大雨使这条小溪淹了公路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 create</td><td>/kriˈeɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>创造，创立</td><td>create a new department<br>创建新部门</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city created a fund to support local artists.  该市设立了一项基金支持本地艺术家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 engender</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒendər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>引起，使发展</td><td>engender trust<br>产生信任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small acts of honesty engender trust more surely than grand promises do.  小小的诚实之举比宏大的承诺更能可靠地产生信任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 generate</td><td>/ˈdʒenəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>产生，引起；发电</td><td>generate interest<br>引起兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trailer generated enormous interest weeks before the release.  预告片在上映数周前就引起了极大兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 induce</td><td>/ɪnˈduːs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>引发，导致</td><td>induce a sense of calm<br>引发平静感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soft music and dim lighting induced a deep sense of calm in everyone.  轻柔的音乐和昏暗的灯光让每个人都产生了深深的平静感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 produce</td><td>/prəˈduːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>生产，制造；产品</td><td>produce steel locally<br>本地生产钢材</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory can produce ten thousand units of steel every month.  这家工厂每月能生产一万单位钢材。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 result</td><td>/rɪˈzʌlt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>结果；导致，产生</td><td>as a result of<br>由于…的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay resulted from a simple error in the schedule.  这次延误源于日程上的一个小错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 suspend</td><td>/səˈspend/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>暂停，中止</td><td>suspend the hearing<br>中止听证会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials had to suspend the search because of the storm.  由于暴风雨，官员们不得不中止搜救。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>irascible</b></td><td>/ɪˈræsəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，暴躁的</td><td>an irascible old man<br>脾气暴躁的老人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The irascible coach shouted at the referee over every call.  这位暴躁的教练对每一次判罚都冲裁判大喊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 choleric</td><td>/ˈkɑːlərɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，暴躁的</td><td>a choleric temper<br>暴躁的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His choleric temper frightened every new employee in the office.  他暴躁的脾气让办公室里每个新员工都害怕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irritable</td><td>/ˈɪrətəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，急躁的</td><td>become irritable and tense<br>变得易怒紧张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lack of sleep makes him irritable by mid-afternoon.  睡眠不足使他到下午中段就变得易怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peevish</td><td>/ˈpiːvɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，爱抱怨的</td><td>a peevish complaint<br>不耐烦的抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She answered in a peevish tone and refused to explain further.  她用不耐烦的语气回答，拒绝再解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 petulant</td><td>/ˈpetʃələnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，爱发脾气的</td><td>a petulant remark<br>使性子的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The actor made a petulant remark and stormed off the stage.  那位演员使性子说了句话，然后气冲冲地离开了舞台。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 affable</td><td>/ˈæfəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和蔼的，温和的</td><td>an affable host<br>和蔼的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our affable neighbor greets everyone on the street by name.  我们和蔼的邻居在街上会叫出每个人的名字打招呼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>irate</b></td><td>/aɪˈreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>极其愤怒的</td><td>an irate customer<br>一位愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An irate customer demanded to speak with the manager immediately.  一位极其愤怒的顾客要求立刻与经理谈话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aggravated</td><td>/ˈæɡrəˌveɪtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>恼怒的；加重的</td><td>aggravated by delays<br>因延误而恼火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sounded aggravated when the call was cut off again.  电话再次中断时他听起来很恼火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apoplectic</td><td>/ˌæpəˈplektɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>暴怒的；中风的</td><td>apoplectic with rage<br>气得暴跳如雷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager was apoplectic when he saw the damage.  经理看到损坏时暴跳如雷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 choleric</td><td>/ˈkɑːlərɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，暴躁的</td><td>a choleric temper<br>暴躁的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His choleric temper frightened every new employee in the office.  他暴躁的脾气让办公室里每个新员工都害怕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enraged</td><td>/ɪnˈreɪdʒd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>愤怒的，被激怒的</td><td>an enraged crowd<br>愤怒的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The enraged customer demanded to speak with the owner.  愤怒的顾客要求与老板对话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exasperated</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæspəreɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>恼怒的，被激怒的</td><td>an exasperated sigh<br>恼怒的叹息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exasperated teacher repeated the instruction a fourth time.  恼火的老师第四次重复了指令。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infuriated</td><td>/ɪnˈfjʊrieɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>被激怒的，狂怒的</td><td>infuriated by the delay<br>因延误而暴怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fans were infuriated when the concert was canceled again.  演唱会再次取消，粉丝们十分愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 ireful</del></td><td>/ˈaɪrfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>愤怒的，充满怒火的</td><td>an ireful glance<br>充满怒火的一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old man gave an ireful look at the noisy boys.  老人愤怒地看了一眼那些吵闹的男孩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mad</td><td>/ˈmæd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>疯狂的，荒唐的；愤怒的</td><td>a mad scheme<br>荒唐的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It would be mad to sell the house at that price.  以那个价格卖房子简直荒唐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wrathful</td><td>/ˈræθfəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>愤怒的，暴怒的</td><td>a wrathful god<br>愤怒的神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poem describes a wrathful sea swallowing the entire fleet.  这首诗描写愤怒的大海吞没了整支舰队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 calm</td><td>/ˈkɑːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、动词、名词</td><td>平静的；使镇静</td><td>calm down quickly<br>很快平静下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a calm voice until the children settled.  她用平静的声音说话，直到孩子们安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 halcyon</td><td>/ˈhælsiən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宁静的，平静的</td><td>halcyon days<br>宁静美好的日子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He often talked about the halcyon days of his youth.  他常谈起年轻时那些宁静美好的日子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>iridescent</b></td><td>/ˌɪrɪˈdesnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>五颜六色的</td><td>iridescent butterfly wings<br>虹彩般的蝶翅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sunlight made the butterfly's iridescent wings flash green, blue, and gold.  阳光让蝴蝶五颜六色的翅膀闪出绿色、蓝色和金色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nacreous</del></td><td>/ˈneɪkriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>珍珠般的，有珠光的</td><td>a nacreous sheen<br>珍珠般的光泽</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shell's nacreous interior glowed softly in the lamplight.  贝壳珠光的内壁在灯下柔柔发亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 opalescent</td><td>/ˌoʊpəˈlesənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乳白闪光的，有虹彩的</td><td>opalescent glass<br>乳白闪光的玻璃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dawn turned the lake water a soft opalescent blue.  黎明把湖水映成柔和的乳光蓝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pearlescent</td><td>/ˌpɜːrˈlesənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>珠光的</td><td>pearlescent nail polish<br>珠光指甲油</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The car's pearlescent paint changed color with the light.  这辆车的珠光漆随光线变色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 monochromatic</td><td>/ˌmɑːnəkroʊˈmætɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单色的</td><td>a monochromatic palette<br>单色的配色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The designer chose a monochromatic scheme of soft grays.  设计师选了柔灰的单色方案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>irk</b></td><td>/ɜːrk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使烦恼，使厌倦</td><td>irk his colleagues<br>使同事厌烦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His habit of humming while working irked everyone in the office.  他工作时哼歌的习惯使办公室里所有人都很厌烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 annoy</td><td>/əˈnɔɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恼怒，不断烦扰</td><td>annoy the neighbors<br>不断打扰邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The barking dog began to annoy every neighbor on the quiet street.  那只不停犬吠的狗开始烦扰这条安静街上的每位邻居。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bother</td><td>/ˈbɑːðər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>打扰，使烦恼；费心</td><td>don't bother me<br>别烦我</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The constant noise from the street really bothers me.  街上不停的噪音真让我烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fret</td><td>/ˈfret/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>烦躁，苦恼</td><td>fret about small things<br>为小事烦恼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She frets constantly about her children's safety at school.  她不停为孩子在校的安全担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gall</td><td>/ɡɔːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（使）焦躁，激怒</td><td>galled by the remark<br>被那句话惹恼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His smug tone galled everyone sitting around the conference table.  他自鸣得意的语气激怒了围坐在会议桌旁的每个人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provoke</td><td>/prəˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒；驱使，激起</td><td>provoke an angry response<br>引发愤怒的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remark provoked an angry response from the whole audience.  他不慎的言论激起了全体观众的愤怒回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ruffle</td><td>/ˈrʌfl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>弄乱（毛发）；扰乱，使恼怒</td><td>ruffle his feathers<br>惹他不快</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single rude question was enough to ruffle the normally calm witness.  一个无礼的问题就足以扰乱这位平素冷静的证人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vex</td><td>/veks/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使烦恼，使恼怒</td><td>vex the teacher<br>使老师恼怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant interruptions vexed the teacher throughout the long afternoon lesson.  不断的打扰让老师在漫长的下午课上十分恼怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 appease</td><td>/əˈpiːz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安抚，平息；满足</td><td>appease the angry crowd<br>安抚愤怒的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered refunds to appease its angry customers.  公司提供退款以安抚愤怒的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pacify</td><td>/ˈpæsɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使平静，安慰</td><td>pacify a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse sang softly to pacify the frightened child before the injection.  打针前，护士轻声唱歌安抚受惊的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 placate</td><td>/ˈpleɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（通过让步以）平息抚慰</td><td>placate the critics<br>平息批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered full refunds to placate its angriest customers.  公司提供全额退款以平息最愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 propitiate</td><td>/prəˈpɪʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抚慰，劝解</td><td>propitiate the angry gods<br>抚慰愤怒的神灵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers offered rice and flowers to propitiate the mountain spirits.  村民献上米和花来抚慰山神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ironclad</b></td><td>/ˈaɪərnklæd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非常坚固的，坚不可摧的</td><td>an ironclad contract<br>无懈可击的合同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyers drafted an ironclad contract that left no room for dispute.  律师起草了一份无懈可击的合同，没有留下争议的空间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 firm</td><td>/ˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的，坚固的</td><td>a firm decision<br>坚定的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remained firm despite enormous pressure from the board.  尽管董事会施加巨大压力，她依然坚定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invulnerable</td><td>/ɪnˈvʌlnərəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不会受伤的，无懈可击的</td><td>invulnerable to attack<br>不受攻击伤害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No fortress is truly invulnerable to a long siege.  没有哪座堡垒真能经受长期围攻而不受损。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 secure</td><td>/sɪˈkjʊr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>牢固的，安全的</td><td>a secure connection<br>安全的连接</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Make sure the ladder is secure before you start climbing.  开始攀爬前确保梯子牢固。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sound</td><td>/saʊnd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>牢固的；健康强壮的；（逻辑）严谨的</td><td>sound financial advice<br>稳妥的理财建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lawyer gave sound financial advice that saved the family thousands.  她的律师给出稳妥的理财建议，为家里省下数千元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tenacious</td><td>/təˈneɪʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>黏性强的；不屈不挠的</td><td>a tenacious opponent<br>顽强的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She proved a tenacious opponent who never conceded a single point.  她是个顽强的对手，从不让出一分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tough</td><td>/ˈtʌf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>艰难的，费力的；坚韧的</td><td>a tough assignment<br>艰巨的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editing that manuscript proved a tough assignment.  编辑那份手稿被证明是项艰巨的任务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fragile</td><td>/ˈfrædʒl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易碎的</td><td>a fragile vase<br>易碎的花瓶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please pack the fragile vase in plenty of soft paper.  请用足够多的软纸把这个易碎的花瓶包好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>irradicable</b></del></td><td>/ɪˈrædɪkəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不能根除的</td><td>an irradicable stain<br>无法去除的污点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty left an irradicable mark on the way he viewed money.  贫穷在他看待金钱的方式上留下了无法根除的印记。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entrenched</td><td>/enˈtrentʃt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>根深蒂固的</td><td>deeply entrenched habits<br>根深蒂固的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Corruption was entrenched at every level of the ministry.  腐败在该部门各个层级根深蒂固。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 ineradicable</del></td><td>/ˌɪnɪˈrædɪkəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不能根除的</td><td>an ineradicable habit<br>无法根除的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Prejudice proved ineradicable despite decades of public education.  尽管数十年公众教育，偏见仍难以根除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ingrained</td><td>/ɪnˈɡreɪnd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>本质的，根深蒂固的</td><td>deeply ingrained habits<br>根深蒂固的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Habits ingrained since childhood are extremely difficult to change in later life.  童年就已根深蒂固的习惯到了晚年极难改变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inveterate</td><td>/ɪnˈvetərət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>根深蒂固的</td><td>an inveterate gambler<br>积习难改的赌徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An inveterate gambler, he lost the family savings within a single year.  他是个积习难改的赌徒，一年之内就输光了家中积蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rooted</td><td>/ˈruːtəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>根深蒂固的，扎根的</td><td>rooted in tradition<br>植根于传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her fear of water was rooted in a childhood accident.  她对水的恐惧源于童年一次事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 eradicable</del></td><td>/ɪˈrædəkəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可根除的，可消灭的</td><td>an eradicable disease<br>可根除的疾病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Smallpox proved eradicable once global vaccination programs began.  一旦全球疫苗接种计划启动，天花被证明是可根除的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>itinerant</b></td><td>/aɪˈtɪnərənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巡游的，巡回的</td><td>an itinerant preacher<br>巡回布道者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An itinerant preacher traveled from village to village on an old bicycle.  一位巡回布道者骑着旧自行车在各村庄之间往来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ambulant</td><td>/ˈæmbjələnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>流动的；能走动的</td><td>an ambulant patient<br>能走动的病人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic treats ambulant patients who do not need a bed.  这家诊所治疗无需住院的能走动病人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 errant</td><td>/ˈerənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>误入歧途的，犯错误的</td><td>an errant husband<br>出轨的丈夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The errant student was suspended after ignoring three separate warnings.  这名行为不端的学生在忽视三次警告后被停学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nomadic</td><td>/noʊˈmædɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>游牧的，流浪的</td><td>a nomadic lifestyle<br>游牧的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her nomadic childhood took her through eleven countries.  她流浪的童年带她走过十一个国家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peripatetic</td><td>/ˌperipəˈtetɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巡游的，四处游历的</td><td>a peripatetic career<br>四处奔波的职业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peripatetic photographer rarely slept in the same city twice.  这位四处游历的摄影师很少在同一座城市住两次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 roaming</td><td>/ˈroʊmɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>漫游的，四处游荡的</td><td>roaming herds of cattle<br>四处游荡的牛群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Roaming bands of musicians played in every square that summer.  那年夏天，漫游的乐队在每个广场演奏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wandering</td><td>/ˈwɑːndərɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>游荡的；散漫的</td><td>a wandering discussion<br>散漫的讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wandering argument never reached any clear conclusion.  这番散漫的论述始终没有明确结论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 4

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>irrigate</b></td><td>/ˈɪrɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>灌溉；冲洗</td><td>irrigate the wound<br>冲洗伤口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse carefully irrigated the wound before applying a clean bandage.  护士仔细冲洗了伤口，然后敷上干净的绷带。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 water</td><td>/ˈwɔːtər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>给…浇水，灌溉</td><td>water the garden<br>给花园浇水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He waters the vegetable beds every morning before sunrise.  他每天日出前给菜地浇水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flush</td><td>/flʌʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、不及物动词</td><td>充裕的，有钱的；冲洗</td><td>flush with money<br>钱很充裕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the bonus he was flush with money for months.  拿到奖金后他有好几个月钱都很充裕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rinse</td><td>/ˈrɪns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>冲洗，漂洗</td><td>rinse the dishes<br>冲洗碗盘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rinse the vegetables under cold running water first.  先用冷流水冲洗蔬菜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wash</td><td>/ˈwɑːʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>冲洗，洗涤</td><td>wash away the dirt<br>冲掉污垢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain washed away the dirt from the steps.  大雨把台阶上的污垢冲掉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
