<tr><td>↳近 join</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，接合；加入</td><td>join the two pieces<br>把两块接起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Join the ends of the rope with a firm knot.  用牢固的结把绳子两端接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 link</td><td>/ˈlɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>联系，纽带；连接</td><td>a close link<br>紧密的联系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a close link between sleep and memory.  研究者发现睡眠与记忆之间存在紧密联系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meld</td><td>/ˈmeld/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>融合，合并</td><td>meld into one<br>融为一体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The choir's voices melded into a single warm sound.  合唱团的声音融合成一种温暖的声响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 merge</td><td>/ˈmɜːrdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>合并，融合</td><td>merge two companies<br>合并两家公司</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two banks merged last year to cut operating costs.  这两家银行去年合并以削减运营成本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tie</td><td>/ˈtaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>纽带，关系；系，绑</td><td>family ties<br>家庭纽带</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strong family ties kept the brothers in the same town.  牢固的家庭纽带让兄弟俩留在同一个镇上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 secure</td><td>/sɪˈkjʊr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>牢固的，安全的</td><td>a secure connection<br>安全的连接</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Make sure the ladder is secure before you start climbing.  开始攀爬前确保梯子牢固。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unite</td><td>/juˈnaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>联合，团结</td><td>unite against a threat<br>联合抵御威胁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crisis united the villagers as nothing else had.  这场危机以前所未有的方式团结了村民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disassociate</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈsoʊʃieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分离，使脱离关系</td><td>disassociate oneself from<br>与…划清界限</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party moved to disassociate itself from the remarks.  该党着手与那些言论划清界限。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disconnect</td><td>/dɪskəˈnekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>断开，使分离</td><td>disconnect the power<br>切断电源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please disconnect the printer before you open its back panel.  打开后盖前请先断开打印机的连接。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disjoin</del></td><td>/dɪsˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使分开，使分离</td><td>disjoin the two parts<br>把两部分分开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineer had to disjoin the pipes to replace a valve.  工程师得把管子分开才能更换阀门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 divide</td><td>/dɪˈvaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分开，划分；分歧</td><td>divide into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher divided the class into four groups of five.  老师把班级分成四个五人小组。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sever</td><td>/ˈsevər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>切断，断绝</td><td>sever all ties<br>断绝一切关系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company severed all ties with its former supplier.  公司与前供应商断绝了一切关系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 split</td><td>/ˈsplɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分裂，分开</td><td>split into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee split into two camps over the proposal.  委员会因该提案分裂成两派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sunder</td><td>/ˈsʌndər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分开，割断</td><td>sunder the alliance<br>使联盟破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bitter dispute sundered an alliance that had lasted decades.  这场激烈的争端割断了维持数十年的联盟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>knotty</b></td><td>/ˈnɑːti/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂的，困难的</td><td>a knotty question<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor posed a knotty question that nobody could answer confidently.  教授提出了一个没人能自信回答的棘手问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baroque</td><td>/bəˈroʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>装饰华丽的，过分雕琢的，复杂的</td><td>baroque ornamentation<br>华丽繁复的装饰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceiling was covered with heavy baroque ornamentation in gold.  天花板上覆满了华丽繁复的金色装饰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 byzantine</td><td>/ˈbɪzəntiːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>错综复杂的</td><td>byzantine bureaucracy<br>错综复杂的官僚体制</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax code is so byzantine that even experts disagree.  税法错综复杂，连专家都各执一词。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complicated</td><td>/ˈkɑːmplɪkeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂的，难懂的</td><td>a complicated process<br>复杂的流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax rules are so complicated that most people hire help.  税务规定如此复杂，多数人都请人帮忙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 convoluted</td><td>/ˈkɑːnvəluːtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂的，费解的</td><td>a convoluted argument<br>费解的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The instructions were so convoluted that nobody could assemble the shelf.  说明书太过费解，没人能装好那个架子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intricate</td><td>/ˈɪntrəkət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>错综复杂的，精细的</td><td>an intricate pattern<br>精细的图案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The carpet shows an intricate pattern of leaves and birds.  地毯上是精细复杂的叶与鸟图案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 involved</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlvd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>错综复杂的，难懂的</td><td>an involved argument<br>错综复杂的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plot is so involved that few readers finish the book.  情节太过错综复杂，很少有读者读完这本书。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 labyrinthine</td><td>/ˌlæbəˈrɪnθaɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迷宫似的，复杂曲折的</td><td>labyrinthine corridors<br>迷宫般的走廊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We got lost in the labyrinthine corridors of the old hospital building.  我们在这座老医院大楼迷宫般的走廊里迷了路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sophisticated</td><td>/səˈfɪstɪkeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂精密的；老练的</td><td>a sophisticated system<br>复杂精密的系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lab uses highly sophisticated equipment to detect the particles.  实验室用极为精密的设备探测这些粒子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tangled</td><td>/ˈtæŋɡəld/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>纠缠的，错综复杂的</td><td>a tangled web<br>错综复杂的关系网</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The case involves a tangled web of loans and false names.  此案涉及贷款与假名交织的复杂网络。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 easy</td><td>/ˈiːzi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易的，不费力的</td><td>an easy question<br>简单的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The homework looked easy, but it took him three hours to finish.  作业看起来容易，可他花了三个小时才做完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 effortless</td><td>/ˈefərtləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻松的，不费力的</td><td>effortless grace<br>毫不费力的优雅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her swimming looked effortless even in the rough water.  即使在波涛中她的游泳也显得毫不费力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plain</td><td>/ˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，十足的；朴素的，清楚的</td><td>plain stupid<br>简直愚蠢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning was just plain stupid.  忽视警告纯粹是愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 simple</td><td>/ˈsɪmpəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简单的，容易的</td><td>a simple task<br>简单的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Follow these simple steps and the machine will restart.  按这些简单步骤操作，机器就会重启。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>kudos</b></td><td>/ˈkuːdoʊz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>名望，名声；夸奖，赞扬</td><td>kudos for the win<br>因获胜而受赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She earned kudos from critics for her fearless debut novel.  她凭无畏的处女作小说赢得了评论界的赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 credit</td><td>/ˈkredɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>表扬，赞扬；信任</td><td>take credit for<br>把功劳归于自己</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She deserves credit for keeping the project alive.  她值得称赞，因为她让这个项目坚持下来了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distinction</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋkʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>殊荣，卓越；区别</td><td>a mark of distinction<br>卓越的标志</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He graduated with distinction from a small rural college.  他以优异成绩从一所乡下的小型学院毕业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 homage</td><td>/ˈhɑːmɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>尊敬，敬意</td><td>pay homage to<br>向…致敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands came to pay homage to the poet on her ninetieth birthday.  数千人在诗人九十岁生日那天前来致敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 honor</td><td>/ˈɑːnər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>尊敬，给以荣誉；荣誉</td><td>honor the veterans<br>表彰老兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city honored the veterans with a quiet morning ceremony.  该市用一场安静的晨间仪式表彰老兵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laurels</td><td>/ˈlɔːrəlz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>荣誉，殊荣</td><td>rest on one's laurels<br>满足于既有荣誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After one prize he rested on his laurels for a decade.  拿过一个奖后他十年都满足于既有荣誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acclaim</td><td>/əˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>称赞</td><td>win critical acclaim<br>赢得评论界赞誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won wide critical acclaim across Europe and Asia.  她的第一部小说在欧洲和亚洲赢得了广泛的评论界赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accolade</td><td>/ˈækəleɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>赞扬，荣誉；奖励</td><td>win the highest accolade<br>赢得最高荣誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel received accolades from critics on both continents.  这部小说获得了两大洲评论家的赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 applause</td><td>/əˈplɔːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>鼓掌；认可，赞许</td><td>a round of applause<br>一阵掌声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her speech was greeted with long and warm applause.  她的演讲赢得了长时间热烈的掌声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laud</td><td>/ˈlɔːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，称赞</td><td>laud his courage<br>赞扬他的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lauded the young pianist for her fearless interpretation.  评论家赞扬这位年轻钢琴家大胆的演绎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 infamy</td><td>/ˈɪnfəmi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恶名，臭名；恶行</td><td>a day of infamy<br>耻辱的一天</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His name lives on in infamy after the scandal.  丑闻之后他的名字以恶名流传下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 notoriety</td><td>/ˌnoʊtəˈraɪəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>坏名声</td><td>gain instant notoriety<br>立刻声名狼藉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The video gained him instant notoriety that followed him for the rest of his career.  这段视频让他立刻声名狼藉，并伴随他整个职业生涯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 belittlement</del></td><td>/bɪˈlɪtlmənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻视，贬低</td><td>constant belittlement<br>不断的贬低</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of constant belittlement had slowly eroded her confidence at work.  多年不断的贬低慢慢侵蚀了她在工作中的自信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 denigration</td><td>/ˌdenɪˈɡreɪʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>诋毁，污蔑</td><td>the denigration of rivals<br>对对手的诋毁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His campaign relied on the denigration of every opponent.  他的竞选依赖于诋毁每一个对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deprecation</td><td>/ˌdeprəˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>贬低，反对</td><td>gentle self-deprecation<br>温和的自我贬低</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her humor is built on gentle self-deprecation rather than cruelty.  她的幽默建立在温和的自我贬低而非刻薄之上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 derogation</td><td>/ˌderəˈɡeɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>贬损，毁损</td><td>derogation of merit<br>对功绩的贬损</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The review was a needless derogation of a fine book.  这篇评论是对一本好书不必要的贬损。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 diminishment</td><td>/dɪˈmɪnɪʃmənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>减少，贬损</td><td>the diminishment of authority<br>权威的削弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Members resented the diminishment of their voting power.  成员们不满其投票权被削弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disparagement</td><td>/dɪˈspærɪdʒmənt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>贬低，轻蔑</td><td>public disparagement<br>公开的贬低</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sued the paper over its disparagement of his research.  他因该报贬低其研究而起诉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>labile</b></td><td>/ˈleɪbaɪl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易变的，不稳定的</td><td>an emotionally labile patient<br>情绪易变的病人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patient's labile mood shifted from cheerful to tearful within minutes.  这位病人易变的情绪几分钟内就从愉快转为流泪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 capricious</td><td>/kəˈprɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>反复无常的，善变的</td><td>capricious weather<br>变化无常的天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The capricious wind kept shifting during the whole race.  整场比赛中变化无常的风一直在转向。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fluctuating</td><td>/ˈflʌktʃuˌeɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>波动的，起伏不定的</td><td>fluctuating prices<br>波动的价格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fluctuating oil prices made it hard to plan next year's budget.  波动的油价让明年的预算难以制定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fluid</td><td>/ˈfluːɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>易变的，不固定的；流体</td><td>a fluid situation<br>变动不定的局势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The schedule remains fluid until the visas finally arrive.  在签证最终到手前，日程仍不固定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inconstant</td><td>/ɪnˈkɑːnstənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易变的，反复无常的</td><td>an inconstant friend<br>反复无常的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The inconstant wind made sailing across the bay nearly impossible.  变化不定的风让横渡海湾几乎无法进行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mercurial</td><td>/mɜːrˈkjʊriəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（情绪）善变的</td><td>a mercurial temper<br>善变的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His mercurial temper made colleagues hesitate before bringing him any bad news.  他善变的脾气让同事在向他报告坏消息前都要犹豫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 temperamental</td><td>/ˌtemprəˈmentəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喜怒无常的；不稳定的</td><td>a temperamental artist<br>喜怒无常的艺术家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old printer is temperamental on humid summer days.  这台旧打印机在潮湿的夏日很不稳定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsettled</td><td>/ʌnˈsetəld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>动荡的，多变的；未定的</td><td>unsettled weather<br>多变的天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unsettled weather ruined all our plans for the weekend.  多变的天气打乱了我们全部周末计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsteady</td><td>/ʌnˈstedi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不稳的，摇晃的</td><td>unsteady hands<br>不稳的手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her voice was unsteady as she read the letter to us.  她给我们读那封信时声音颤抖不稳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 variable</td><td>/ˈveriəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>可变的，多变的</td><td>variable costs<br>可变成本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Winds in the strait are variable and often dangerous.  海峡里的风多变且常常危险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 volatile</td><td>/ˈvɑːlətl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>多变的</td><td>a volatile stock market<br>多变的股市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investors hesitated because the stock market stayed volatile all spring.  由于股市整个春天都动荡多变，投资者犹豫不决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 constant</td><td>/ˈkɑːnstənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不变的，恒定的；忠诚的</td><td>a constant friend<br>忠诚不变的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her support remained constant through every difficult year.  她的支持在每个艰难年头都不曾改变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immutable</td><td>/ɪˈmjuːtəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可变的</td><td>an immutable law of nature<br>不可变的自然法则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gravity is an immutable law that no clever argument can suspend.  引力是不可变的法则，任何巧辩都无法使它失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invariable</td><td>/ɪnˈveriəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不变的，恒定的</td><td>an invariable routine<br>一成不变的常规</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His morning routine has been invariable for twenty years.  他的晨间习惯二十年来一成不变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stable</td><td>/ˈsteɪbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>稳定的；马厩</td><td>a stable income<br>稳定的收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His condition has been stable since the operation last Tuesday.  自上周二手术以来他的病情一直稳定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stationary</td><td>/ˈsteɪʃəˌneri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>静止的，固定不动的</td><td>remain stationary<br>保持静止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic on the bridge stayed stationary for almost two hours.  桥上的车流几乎两小时一动不动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 steady</td><td>/ˈstedi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稳定的，持续不变的</td><td>a steady stream of visitors<br>络绎不绝的来访者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sales have shown steady growth for eight straight quarters.  销售额已连续八个季度稳定增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>laborious</b></td><td>/ləˈbɔːriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>费力的，艰苦的；勤奋的</td><td>a laborious process<br>费力的过程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Copying the manuscript by hand proved a slow and laborious process.  手抄这份手稿是一个缓慢而费力的过程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 active</td><td>/ˈæktɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活跃的，活动的</td><td>an active volcano<br>活火山</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stays active by swimming every morning.  她每天早上游泳以保持活跃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assiduous</td><td>/əˈsɪdʒuəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>勤勉的，专心仔细的</td><td>assiduous attention to detail<br>对细节的专注仔细</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her assiduous attention to detail saved the project from disaster.  她对细节的专注仔细使项目免于失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bustling</td><td>/ˈbʌslɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熙熙攘攘的，忙碌的</td><td>a bustling market<br>熙熙攘攘的集市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The port grew into a bustling center of trade.  这座港口发展成了繁忙的贸易中心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diligent</td><td>/ˈdɪlɪdʒənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>勤勉的，辛勤的</td><td>a diligent student<br>勤奋的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A diligent editor checked every footnote before the manuscript went to the printer.  一位勤勉的编辑在书稿付印前核对了每一条脚注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 engaged</td><td>/ɪnˈɡeɪdʒd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>忙于…的；已订婚的</td><td>engaged in research<br>忙于研究工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was engaged in fieldwork throughout the entire rainy season.  整个雨季她都忙于野外调查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 industrious</td><td>/ɪnˈdʌstriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>勤勉的，刻苦的</td><td>an industrious worker<br>勤劳的工人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The most industrious student in the class reviewed her notes every single night.  班里最勤奋的学生每晚都复习笔记。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 occupied</td><td>/ˈɑːkjəˌpaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忙于…的，被占用的</td><td>fully occupied with work<br>完全忙于工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was occupied with the report all afternoon.  她整个下午都忙于那份报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 sedulous</del></td><td>/ˈsedʒələs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>勤奋认真的</td><td>sedulous preparation<br>勤勉认真的准备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her sedulous preparation showed in every answer she gave the examiners.  她勤勉认真的准备体现在给考官的每个回答中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arduous</td><td>/ˈɑːrdʒuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以做到的，费劲的</td><td>an arduous journey<br>艰苦的旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The climb proved far more arduous than any of them expected.  这次攀登远比他们任何人预想的都费劲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 challenging</td><td>/ˈtʃæləndʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有挑战性的，费心力的</td><td>a challenging assignment<br>有挑战性的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor gave us a challenging assignment due next week.  教授给了我们一个下周交的有挑战性作业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demanding</td><td>/dɪˈmændɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难取悦的，难满足的；费时间花心思的</td><td>a demanding new job<br>费心费力的新工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Caring for three small children is demanding work with no holidays.  照顾三个小孩是费时间花心思且没有休假的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 difficult</td><td>/ˈdɪfəkəlt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>困难的，费力的</td><td>a difficult climb<br>艰难的攀登</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The final section of the trail is genuinely difficult.  这条小径的最后一段确实艰难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exacting</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæktɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，苛求的；费时间花心思的</td><td>exacting quality standards<br>严格的质量标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The airline maintains exacting standards for every part it installs.  这家航空公司对安装的每个部件都保持严格的标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 formidable</td><td>/ˈfɔːrmɪdəbl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以对付的，令人敬畏的</td><td>a formidable opponent<br>难以对付的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She faced a formidable opponent in the final round.  她在最后一轮遇到了难以对付的对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grueling</td><td>/ˈɡruːəlɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>累人的，折磨人的</td><td>a grueling schedule<br>折磨人的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team endured a grueling schedule of twelve games in three weeks.  球队熬过了三周十二场比赛这种折磨人的赛程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heavy</td><td>/ˈhevi/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重的，沉重的</td><td>too heavy to lift<br>重得抬不动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The box was so heavy that two men could barely move it.  这箱子太重，两个人几乎搬不动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 labored</td><td>/ˈleɪbərd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>费力的，不自然的</td><td>labored breathing<br>费力的呼吸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His labored breathing worried the paramedics on the way to hospital.  他费力的呼吸让送医途中的急救人员很担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rigorous</td><td>/ˈrɪɡərəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（天气）严酷的；严格的</td><td>a rigorous climate<br>严酷的气候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Few crops survive the rigorous winters of the plateau.  很少有作物能度过高原严酷的冬天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rough</td><td>/ˈrʌf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗糙的，粗暴的；艰苦的</td><td>a rough surface<br>粗糙的表面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The road grew rough as we climbed into the hills.  我们爬上山时路变得坎坷不平。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rugged</td><td>/ˈrʌɡəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚毅的；粗糙不平的</td><td>rugged terrain<br>险峻的地形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only rugged travelers attempt that trail in the winter.  只有意志坚毅的旅行者才会在冬天走那条小径。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 severe</td><td>/səˈvɪr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的；严重的</td><td>a severe penalty<br>严厉的处罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge imposed a severe penalty on the repeat offender.  法官对累犯施以严厉处罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strenuous</td><td>/ˈstrenjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>费力的，艰巨的；奋力的</td><td>strenuous exercise<br>费力的运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors advised him to avoid strenuous exercise for at least a month.  医生建议他至少一个月内避免费力的运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sweaty</td><td>/ˈsweti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>费力的，累人的；出汗的</td><td>sweaty work<br>累人的活儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Moving the piano upstairs was hot, sweaty work.  把钢琴搬上楼是又热又累人的活儿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 toilsome</del></td><td>/ˈtɔɪlsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>辛苦的，费力的</td><td>toilsome labor<br>辛苦的劳作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Copying manuscripts by hand was toilsome work that ruined many eyes.  手抄书稿是毁坏许多人眼睛的辛苦活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tough</td><td>/ˈtʌf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>艰难的，费力的；坚韧的</td><td>a tough assignment<br>艰巨的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editing that manuscript proved a tough assignment.  编辑那份手稿被证明是项艰巨的任务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 idle</td><td>/ˈaɪdl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闲置的，未使用的；懒散的</td><td>idle machines<br>闲置的机器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Half the factory's machines stood idle for most of last year.  这家工厂一半的机器去年大部分时间都闲置着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inactive</td><td>/ɪnˈæktɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不活动的，不活跃的</td><td>remain inactive for months<br>数月毫无动作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The volcano has been inactive for over two hundred years.  这座火山已有两百多年没有活动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indolent</td><td>/ˈɪndələnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>懒惰的</td><td>an indolent young man<br>懒散的年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too indolent to look for work, he simply lay on the couch all summer.  他懒得去找工作，整个夏天就躺在沙发上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inert</td><td>/ɪˈnɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（人）懒惰缺乏活力的；（物品）惰性的</td><td>an inert gas<br>惰性气体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Helium is an inert gas, so it rarely reacts with other elements.  氦是一种惰性气体，因此很少与其他元素发生反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 slothful</td><td>/ˈsloʊθfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>懒惰的，迟缓的</td><td>slothful habits<br>懒散的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of easy living had made the once-diligent clerk slothful.  多年安逸生活使这位曾经勤勉的职员变得懒散。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 easy</td><td>/ˈiːzi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易的，不费力的</td><td>an easy question<br>简单的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The homework looked easy, but it took him three hours to finish.  作业看起来容易，可他花了三个小时才做完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 effortless</td><td>/ˈefərtləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻松的，不费力的</td><td>effortless grace<br>毫不费力的优雅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her swimming looked effortless even in the rough water.  即使在波涛中她的游泳也显得毫不费力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 facile</td><td>/ˈfæsl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易做到的；肤浅轻率的</td><td>a facile explanation<br>肤浅的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report offers a facile explanation for a very complex problem.  这份报告对一个非常复杂的问题给出了肤浅的解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 light</td><td>/ˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻微的，不严重的</td><td>a light meal<br>一顿便餐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He suffered only light injuries in the accident.  他在事故中只受了轻伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mindless</td><td>/ˈmaɪndləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不需用脑的；愚蠢的</td><td>mindless routine work<br>不需用脑的例行工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted mindless work that let him rest his brain.  他想做点不用脑的活儿，让大脑休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 simple</td><td>/ˈsɪmpəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简单的，容易的</td><td>a simple task<br>简单的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Follow these simple steps and the machine will restart.  按这些简单步骤操作，机器就会重启。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undemanding</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈmændɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>要求不高的，轻松的</td><td>an undemanding course<br>要求不高的课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She chose an undemanding role so she could rest more.  她选了个轻松的角色以便多休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>labyrinthine</b></td><td>/ˌlæbəˈrɪnθaɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迷宫似的，复杂曲折的</td><td>labyrinthine corridors<br>迷宫般的走廊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We got lost in the labyrinthine corridors of the old hospital building.  我们在这座老医院大楼迷宫般的走廊里迷了路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baroque</td><td>/bəˈroʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>装饰华丽的，过分雕琢的，复杂的</td><td>baroque ornamentation<br>华丽繁复的装饰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceiling was covered with heavy baroque ornamentation in gold.  天花板上覆满了华丽繁复的金色装饰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 byzantine</td><td>/ˈbɪzəntiːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>错综复杂的</td><td>byzantine bureaucracy<br>错综复杂的官僚体制</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax code is so byzantine that even experts disagree.  税法错综复杂，连专家都各执一词。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complicate</td><td>/ˈkɑːmplɪkeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复杂化</td><td>complicate matters further<br>使事情更复杂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence complicated the investigation and delayed the final report.  新证据使调查复杂化，并推迟了最终报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complicated</td><td>/ˈkɑːmplɪkeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂的，难懂的</td><td>a complicated process<br>复杂的流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax rules are so complicated that most people hire help.  税务规定如此复杂，多数人都请人帮忙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 convoluted</td><td>/ˈkɑːnvəluːtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂的，费解的</td><td>a convoluted argument<br>费解的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The instructions were so convoluted that nobody could assemble the shelf.  说明书太过费解，没人能装好那个架子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intricate</td><td>/ˈɪntrəkət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>错综复杂的，精细的</td><td>an intricate pattern<br>精细的图案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The carpet shows an intricate pattern of leaves and birds.  地毯上是精细复杂的叶与鸟图案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 involved</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlvd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>错综复杂的，难懂的</td><td>an involved argument<br>错综复杂的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plot is so involved that few readers finish the book.  情节太过错综复杂，很少有读者读完这本书。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 knotty</td><td>/ˈnɑːti/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂的，困难的</td><td>a knotty question<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor posed a knotty question that nobody could answer confidently.  教授提出了一个没人能自信回答的棘手问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sophisticated</td><td>/səˈfɪstɪkeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂精密的；老练的</td><td>a sophisticated system<br>复杂精密的系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lab uses highly sophisticated equipment to detect the particles.  实验室用极为精密的设备探测这些粒子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tangled</td><td>/ˈtæŋɡəld/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>纠缠的，错综复杂的</td><td>a tangled web<br>错综复杂的关系网</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The case involves a tangled web of loans and false names.  此案涉及贷款与假名交织的复杂网络。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 easy</td><td>/ˈiːzi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易的，不费力的</td><td>an easy question<br>简单的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The homework looked easy, but it took him three hours to finish.  作业看起来容易，可他花了三个小时才做完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 effortless</td><td>/ˈefərtləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻松的，不费力的</td><td>effortless grace<br>毫不费力的优雅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her swimming looked effortless even in the rough water.  即使在波涛中她的游泳也显得毫不费力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plain</td><td>/ˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，十足的；朴素的，清楚的</td><td>plain stupid<br>简直愚蠢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning was just plain stupid.  忽视警告纯粹是愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 simple</td><td>/ˈsɪmpəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简单的，容易的</td><td>a simple task<br>简单的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Follow these simple steps and the machine will restart.  按这些简单步骤操作，机器就会重启。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lacerate</b></td><td>/ˈlæsəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使非常痛苦</td><td>lacerate someone's feelings<br>深深伤害某人感情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remarks lacerated her feelings more deeply than he realized.  他随口的话比他意识到的更深地伤害了她的感情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 afflict</td><td>/əˈflɪkt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使痛苦，折磨</td><td>be afflicted with doubt<br>被疑虑折磨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Drought has afflicted the region for three straight years.  干旱已连续三年折磨这个地区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distress</td><td>/dɪsˈtres/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>痛苦，忧伤；使痛苦</td><td>cause great distress<br>造成极大痛苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden news distressed everyone in the small office.  突来的消息让小办公室里的每个人都很难受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harrow</td><td>/ˈhæroʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>折磨，使苦恼</td><td>harrow his conscience<br>折磨他的良心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The memory of that night harrowed him for the rest of his life.  那一夜的记忆折磨了他的余生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hurt</td><td>/ˈhɜːrt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>伤害，使疼痛</td><td>hurt his feelings<br>伤害他的感情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your careless comment clearly hurt her feelings at the dinner.  你随口的评论显然在晚餐上伤了她的感情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rend</td><td>/rend/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撕裂，猛拉</td><td>rend the cloth in two<br>把布撕成两半</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The angry protester tried to rend the banner into two ragged halves.  那名愤怒的抗议者试图把横幅撕成破烂的两半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torment</td><td>/tɔːrˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，使痛苦；痛苦</td><td>torment the prisoners<br>折磨囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guilt tormented him for years after the accident.  事故之后，负罪感折磨了他多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torture</td><td>/ˈtɔːrtʃər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，拷打；酷刑</td><td>under torture<br>在酷刑之下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long wait for results was pure torture for her.  漫长等待结果对她来说简直是折磨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wound</td><td>/wuːnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>伤害，使受伤</td><td>wound someone's pride<br>伤害某人的自尊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless joke wounded her more than he realized.  他随口的玩笑对她的伤害超出他的想象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 allay</td><td>/əˈleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，平息</td><td>allay public fears<br>平息公众恐慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statement was meant to allay concerns about safety.  这份声明意在减轻人们对安全的担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 alleviate</td><td>/əˈliːvieɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缓和，减轻</td><td>alleviate the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A warm bath helped alleviate the soreness in his back.  热水澡帮他缓解了背部的酸痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ease</td><td>/ˈiːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减轻，缓和；容易</td><td>ease the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The medicine eased her headache within minutes of the first dose.  这药在服下第一剂几分钟内就缓解了她的头痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mitigate</td><td>/ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，使缓和</td><td>mitigate the damage<br>减轻损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Planting trees can mitigate the effects of summer heat.  种树可以减轻夏季高温的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mollify</td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 palliate</del></td><td>/ˈpælieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓和；掩饰</td><td>palliate the symptoms<br>缓解症状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new drug can only palliate the pain, not cure the disease.  这种新药只能减轻疼痛，无法治愈疾病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relieve</td><td>/rɪˈliːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓解；换班替换</td><td>relieve the pressure<br>缓解压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk relieved the tension in her shoulders.  短暂散步缓解了她肩膀的紧张。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lackluster</b></td><td>/ˈlæklʌstər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>黯淡无光泽的</td><td>a lackluster performance<br>平淡无光的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lackluster hair and tired face worried everyone at home.  她黯淡的头发和疲惫的脸让家人都担心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dim</td><td>/ˈdɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>暗淡的，模糊的</td><td>a dim light<br>昏暗的灯光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The room was too dim for reading anything but large print.  房间太暗，只能看大字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dull</td><td>/dʌl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的；乏味的</td><td>a dull student<br>迟钝的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lecture was so dull that half the class slept.  讲座乏味得让一半学生睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flat</td><td>/ˈflæt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调无光的，乏味的</td><td>a flat performance<br>平淡无味的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paint dried to a flat, lifeless gray.  油漆干成了一种无光而死板的灰色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 lusterless</del></td><td>/ˈlʌstərləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无光泽的，暗淡的</td><td>lusterless hair<br>无光泽的头发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old coins were lusterless but perfectly preserved.  这些旧硬币虽无光泽却保存完好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 burnished</td><td>/ˈbɜːrnɪʃt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>擦亮的，有光泽的</td><td>burnished copper<br>擦亮的铜器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The burnished brass handle shone brightly in the afternoon sunlight.  擦亮的铜把手在午后阳光下闪亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glistening</td><td>/ˈɡlɪsənɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闪光的，闪亮的</td><td>glistening wet streets<br>湿漉发亮的街道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Glistening drops of rain hung from every bare branch.  闪亮的雨珠挂在每一根光枝上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glossy</td><td>/ˈɡlɑːsi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平滑的，有光泽的</td><td>glossy hair<br>有光泽的头发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine printed her photograph on thick and glossy paper.  杂志把她的照片印在厚实而有光泽的纸上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lustrous</td><td>/ˈlʌstrəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有光泽的</td><td>lustrous black hair<br>有光泽的黑发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lustrous black hair shone under the bright lights of the stage.  她有光泽的黑发在明亮的舞台灯光下闪耀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 polished</td><td>/ˈpɑːlɪʃt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的</td><td>polished manners<br>优雅得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His polished manners impressed everyone at the formal dinner.  他优雅得体的举止让正式晚宴上的每个人都印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shiny</td><td>/ˈʃaɪni/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有光泽的，闪亮的</td><td>shiny new shoes<br>闪亮的新鞋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He polished the table until it was shiny.  他把桌子擦得闪亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sleek</td><td>/sliːk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>时髦的，优美的</td><td>a sleek silver car<br>线条优美的银色轿车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She drove a sleek silver car that turned heads on every street.  她开着一辆线条优美的银色轿车，一路引人注目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 8

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>laconic</b></td><td>/ləˈkɑːnɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简洁（以至于显得粗鲁或难以理解）的</td><td>a laconic reply<br>简短生硬的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His laconic reply told us nothing about what had actually happened.  他简短生硬的回答没有告诉我们实际发生了什么。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 apothegmatic</del></td><td>/ˌæpəθeɡˈmætɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>格言式的，简练精辟的</td><td>an apothegmatic style<br>格言式的文风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His apothegmatic prose fits neatly on a postcard.  他格言式的文字正好能写满一张明信片。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brief</td><td>/ˈbriːf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简短的，简洁的</td><td>a brief summary<br>简短的总结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a brief account of the accident to the police.  他向警方简短叙述了事故经过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 capsule</td><td>/ˈkæpsəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>胶囊；压缩的，简要的</td><td>a capsule summary<br>简要概述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor asked for a capsule version of the long report.  编辑要一份长篇报告的浓缩版本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compact</td><td>/ˈkɑːmpækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>契约，协议；紧凑的</td><td>sign a compact<br>签订协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two nations signed a compact to protect the shared river.  两国签订协议保护共有的河流。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compendious</td><td>/kəmˈpɛndiəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简明扼要的</td><td>a compendious reference guide<br>简明扼要的参考手册</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wrote a compendious history of the city in ninety pages.  他用九十页写成了一部简明的城市史。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 curt</td><td>/kɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>言词简略的，直接（以至显得粗鲁）的</td><td>a curt answer<br>简慢的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her curt reply made it clear that the discussion was over.  她简慢的回答清楚表明讨论已经结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pithy</td><td>/ˈpɪθi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简练的，言简意赅的</td><td>a pithy remark<br>精练的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her pithy summary captured the whole debate in two sentences.  她简练的总结用两句话概括了整场辩论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 succinct</td><td>/səkˈsɪŋkt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简明的，简洁的</td><td>a succinct summary<br>简洁的总结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her succinct summary covered the entire report in three short paragraphs.  她简洁的总结用三小段概括了整份报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 summary</td><td>/ˈsʌməri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>概括的，简略的；摘要</td><td>a summary of the report<br>报告的摘要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please attach a one-page summary to the front of the proposal.  请在提案前面附上一页摘要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 telegraphic</td><td>/ˌtelɪˈɡræfɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简短扼要的，电报式的</td><td>a telegraphic reply<br>简短扼要的回复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sent a telegraphic note: arriving Tuesday, bring keys.  她发来一条电报式的短信：周二到，带钥匙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 terse</td><td>/tɜːrs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简洁的，简明的</td><td>a terse reply<br>简洁的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His terse note said only that the deal had collapsed.  他简短的字条只说交易破裂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 circuitous</td><td>/sərˈkjuːɪtəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迂回的，不直接的</td><td>a circuitous route<br>迂回的路线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We took a circuitous route to avoid the flooded highway.  我们绕了一条迂回的路以避开被淹的公路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 circumlocutory</del></td><td>/ˌsɜrkəmˈlɑkjətɔri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迂回冗赘的，绕弯子的</td><td>circumlocutory replies<br>绕弯子的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His circumlocutory answers hid a very simple refusal.  他绕弯子的答复掩盖了一个简单的拒绝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 diffuse</td><td>/dɪfˈjuːs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长散漫的；扩散的</td><td>a diffuse and wordy essay<br>散漫冗长的文章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument was far too diffuse to convince a careful reader.  他的论证过于散漫，无法说服细心的读者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prolix</td><td>/ˈproʊlɪks/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>啰嗦的，冗长的</td><td>prolix legal prose<br>冗长的法律文字</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge criticized the prolix brief for burying its argument.  法官批评这份冗长的书状埋没了自己的论点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rambling</td><td>/ˈræmblɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>漫无目的的，散漫的</td><td>a rambling explanation<br>散漫的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a rambling answer that satisfied nobody present.  他给出散漫的回答，在场无人满意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 verbose</td><td>/vɜːrˈboʊs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长的，啰嗦的</td><td>a verbose explanation<br>冗长的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her verbose report could have said the same thing in two pages.  她冗长的报告本可以用两页说完同样的内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 windy</td><td>/ˈwɪndi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长的</td><td>a windy speech<br>冗长空洞的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His windy speech said very little in more than an hour.  他冗长的演讲讲了一个多小时却几乎没什么内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wordy</td><td>/ˈwɜːrdi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长的，啰嗦的</td><td>a wordy introduction<br>啰嗦的引言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor cut the wordy opening by half.  编辑把啰嗦的开头删掉一半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lachrymose</b></td><td>/ˈlækrɪmoʊs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>催人泪下的，悲伤的</td><td>a lachrymose farewell<br>催人泪下的告别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film's lachrymose ending left half the theater reaching for tissues.  这部电影催人泪下的结尾让半个影院的人去找纸巾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doleful</td><td>/ˈdoʊlfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲哀的，忧郁的</td><td>a doleful voice<br>悲哀的语调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She told the whole story in a low doleful voice.  她用低沉悲哀的语调讲完了整件事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lamentable</td><td>/ləˈmentəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人惋惜的，可悲的</td><td>a lamentable failure<br>令人痛惜的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The condition of the old school building is truly lamentable.  这所旧校舍的状况实在令人惋惜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lugubrious</td><td>/ləˈɡuːbriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（故作夸张的）悲哀的</td><td>a lugubrious expression<br>夸张悲哀的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wore a lugubrious expression whenever anyone mentioned his ruined garden.  每次有人提到他毁掉的花园，他就露出夸张悲哀的表情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 melancholy</td><td>/ˈmelənkɑːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>忧郁，伤感</td><td>a mood of quiet melancholy<br>淡淡的忧郁情绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Autumn rain always fills him with a vague melancholy.  秋雨总让他心里泛起一种莫名的忧郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mournful</td><td>/ˈmɔːrnfl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲伤的</td><td>a mournful melody<br>悲伤的旋律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A mournful melody drifted from the church as the procession passed by.  送葬队伍经过时，教堂里飘出一段悲伤的旋律。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tearful</td><td>/ˈtɪrfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>含泪的，悲伤的</td><td>a tearful reunion<br>含泪的重逢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They said a tearful goodbye at the station gate.  他们在车站门口含泪告别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 teary</td><td>/ˈtɪri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>泪汪汪的，爱哭的</td><td>teary eyes<br>泪汪汪的眼睛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew teary at the mention of her old dog.  提到她的老狗时她泪汪汪的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weepy</td><td>/ˈwiːpi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>爱哭的，伤感的</td><td>a weepy film<br>催泪的电影</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I get weepy every time that song plays.  每次那首歌响起我就想哭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 woeful</td><td>/ˈwoʊfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲惨的，糟糕的</td><td>a woeful performance<br>糟糕的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team's woeful defending cost them the championship again.  球队糟糕的防守又一次让他们丢了冠军。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cheerful</td><td>/ˈtʃɪrfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快乐的，愉快的</td><td>a cheerful greeting<br>愉快的问候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed cheerful even after the long delay.  即使长时间延误后他仍保持愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delighted</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪtəd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高兴的，欣喜的</td><td>delighted with the gift<br>对礼物很欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was delighted to hear about her friend's promotion.  听说朋友升职，她非常高兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 jocund</del></td><td>/ˈdʒɑːkənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>欢快的，高兴的</td><td>a jocund gathering<br>欢快的聚会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The jocund crowd sang and danced until the last lantern went out.  欢快的人群唱歌跳舞，直到最后一盏灯笼熄灭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 jovial</td><td>/ˈdʒoʊviəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>愉快的</td><td>a jovial host<br>愉快的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our jovial host kept the guests laughing throughout the long winter evening.  我们愉快的主人让客人们在漫长的冬夜里笑声不断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lambaste</b></td><td>/læmˈbeɪst/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉斥责，痛骂</td><td>lambaste the government<br>痛斥政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lambasted the film for its shallow, sentimental ending.  批评家严厉斥责这部电影结局浅薄而煽情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assail</td><td>/əˈseɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抨击，严厉批评</td><td>assail a policy<br>抨击某项政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Opponents assailed the plan as reckless and unfair.  反对者抨击该计划轻率且不公。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baste</td><td>/ˈbeɪst/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>痛打；严厉批评</td><td>baste him with criticism<br>用批评痛击他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The columnist basted the mayor for wasting so much public money.  专栏作家痛批市长浪费了大量公款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 belabor</td><td>/bɪˈleɪbər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>过分强调；痛打</td><td>belabor the point<br>反复纠缠一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is no need to belabor the point any further.  没有必要再对这一点过分强调下去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 berate</td><td>/bɪˈreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉指责，斥责</td><td>berate him publicly<br>公开斥责他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach began to berate his players in front of reporters.  教练开始在记者面前严厉斥责他的球员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 castigate</td><td>/ˈkæstɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强烈（公开）指责</td><td>castigate for negligence<br>因疏忽而受斥责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge castigated the company for its negligence.  法官严厉指责该公司的疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excoriate</td><td>/ɪkˈskɔːrieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉批评；擦伤皮肤</td><td>excoriate the government<br>严厉批评政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics excoriated the film for its casual cruelty.  批评家严厉批评该片的随意残忍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reprimand</td><td>/ˈreprəˌmænd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>训斥，正式谴责</td><td>reprimand an employee<br>训斥员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was reprimanded for leaving his post early.  这名警官因提早离岗而受到训斥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reproach</td><td>/rɪˈproʊtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批评，责备</td><td>reproach oneself for<br>因某事自责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She reproached him for never returning her calls.  她责备他从不回她的电话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 scathe</del></td><td>/skeɪð/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉批评；伤害</td><td>scathe with bitter sarcasm<br>用讽刺严厉批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reviewers scathed the film for its lazy writing and dull acting.  评论者严厉批评这部电影剧本敷衍、表演乏味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slam</td><td>/ˈslæm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉批评；猛击</td><td>slam the proposal<br>猛烈批评该提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics slammed the film for its careless plotting.  评论家因情节草率而猛烈批评这部电影。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 upbraid</del></td><td>/ʌpˈbreɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（严厉地）谴责，责骂</td><td>upbraid him for lying<br>因撒谎而责骂他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager upbraided him for missing three deadlines in one month.  经理因他一个月内三次误期而严厉责骂他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 vituperate</del></td><td>/vaɪˈtuːpəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谩骂，责骂</td><td>vituperate an opponent<br>谩骂对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The columnist vituperated the mayor in every weekly article.  这位专栏作家在每周的文章里都痛骂市长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 carol</td><td>/ˈkærəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>欢快颂唱，歌颂；颂歌</td><td>carol his praises<br>歌颂他的功绩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children caroled cheerfully outside every door on the street.  孩子们在街上每户门前欢快地颂唱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extol</td><td>/ɪkˈstoʊl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美；吹捧</td><td>extol his virtues<br>赞美他的品德</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors still extol his kindness during the long winter flood.  邻居们至今仍赞美他在漫长冬季洪水期间的善举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glorify</td><td>/ˈɡlɔːrəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，美化</td><td>glorify war and violence<br>美化战争与暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics said the film glorified war instead of questioning it.  批评者说这部电影美化战争而非质疑它。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hymn</td><td>/hɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>赞歌，赞美诗</td><td>sing a hymn<br>唱赞美诗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole congregation stood up to sing a hymn of thanksgiving.  全体教众站起身来唱一首感恩的赞美诗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 laud</td><td>/ˈlɔːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，称赞</td><td>laud his courage<br>赞扬他的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lauded the young pianist for her fearless interpretation.  评论家赞扬这位年轻钢琴家大胆的演绎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 magnify</td><td>/ˈmæɡnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放大；夸大</td><td>magnify the problem<br>夸大问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lens magnifies the specimen four hundred times.  这个镜头把样本放大四百倍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 praise</td><td>/ˈpreɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>赞扬，称赞</td><td>win high praise<br>获得高度赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won high praise from serious critics.  她的第一部小说赢得了严肃评论家的高度赞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lament</b></td><td>/ləˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>为…哀悼，表达痛苦或遗憾</td><td>lament the loss<br>哀叹损失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Older residents still lament the loss of the orchards that once surrounded town.  年长的居民仍在哀叹曾环绕小镇的果园的消失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bemoan</td><td>/bɪˈmoʊn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>哀叹，抱怨</td><td>bemoan his bad luck<br>哀叹自己运气不好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Farmers bemoaned the loss of their crops after the flood.  洪水过后农民哀叹作物尽毁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deplore</td><td>/dɪˈplɔːr/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谴责，强烈反对</td><td>deplore the violence<br>谴责暴力行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Church leaders deplored the violence and called for immediate restraint.  教会领袖谴责暴力行为，呼吁立即克制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grieve</td><td>/ɡriːv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>（使）感到悲伤</td><td>grieve for a lost friend<br>为逝去的朋友悲伤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village grieved for the fishermen lost in the autumn storm.  全村人都为在秋季风暴中失踪的渔民而悲伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moan</td><td>/ˈmoʊn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>哀叹，抱怨</td><td>moan about the weather<br>抱怨天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They moaned about the heat for the entire drive.  整段车程他们都在抱怨炎热。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mourn</td><td>/ˈmɔːrn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>哀悼，悲伤</td><td>mourn the dead<br>哀悼死者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village mourned the fishermen lost at sea.  全村哀悼在海上失踪的渔民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wail</td><td>/ˈweɪl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>痛哭，哀号；抱怨</td><td>wail in grief<br>悲痛地哀号</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mourners wailed as the coffin was lowered into the grave.  棺木下葬时，吊唁者们放声哀号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delight</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使高兴，使愉快；愉悦</td><td>delight the audience<br>使观众高兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist delighted the audience with a lively encore.  这位年轻钢琴家用一首活泼的加演曲取悦了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exult</td><td>/ɪɡˈzʌlt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>感到欢喜</td><td>exult in her victory<br>为她的胜利而欢喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fans exulted in the victory long after the final whistle.  终场哨响很久之后球迷们仍在为胜利欢喜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 joy</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>欢乐，喜悦</td><td>jump for joy<br>高兴得跳起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news of his recovery filled the whole family with joy.  他康复的消息让全家人充满喜悦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rejoice</td><td>/rɪˈdʒɔɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>欣喜，喜悦</td><td>rejoice at the news<br>为消息而欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village rejoiced at the news of the safe return.  听到平安归来的消息，全村都欣喜不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>libelous</b></td><td>/ˈlaɪbələs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>恶意中伤的，诽谤的</td><td>a libelous accusation<br>诽谤性的指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The newspaper apologized after printing a libelous accusation against the mayor.  报社在刊登了对市长诽谤性的指控后道了歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 backbiting</td><td>/ˈbækˌbaɪtɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>背后诽谤，说人坏话</td><td>office backbiting<br>办公室里的背后诽谤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant backbiting slowly poisoned the atmosphere in the whole department.  不断的背后诽谤慢慢毒害了整个部门的气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 calumnious</del></td><td>/kəˈlʌmniəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>诽谤的，中伤的</td><td>calumnious rumors<br>诽谤性的谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He denied the calumnious claims in a public letter.  他在一封公开信中否认了那些诽谤性说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defamatory</td><td>/dɪˈfæməˌtɔːri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>诽谤的，损害名誉的</td><td>defamatory statements<br>诽谤性的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court ruled that the anonymous blog post was defamatory.  法院裁定那篇匿名博文构成诽谤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 detractive</del></td><td>/dɪˈtræktɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贬损的，中伤的</td><td>detractive remarks<br>贬损的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her detractive comments about the design surprised us.  她对设计的贬损评论让我们意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invidious</td><td>/ɪnˈvɪdiəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>惹人反感的；羡慕嫉妒恨的</td><td>an invidious comparison<br>招人反感的比较</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ranking the students publicly created invidious comparisons that poisoned the whole class.  公开给学生排名制造了招人反感的比较，毒害了整个班级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maligning</td><td>/məˈlaɪnɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>诽谤，恶意中伤</td><td>maligning a rival's character<br>中伤对手的人品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Maligning your competitors rarely wins you the trust of voters.  中伤竞争对手很少能赢得选民的信任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scandalous</td><td>/ˈskændələs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引起反感的，丢脸的</td><td>a scandalous waste of money<br>令人反感的浪费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics called the project a scandalous waste of public money.  批评者称这个项目是对公共资金令人反感的浪费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slanderous</td><td>/ˈslændərəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>诽谤的，造谣中伤的</td><td>slanderous gossip<br>造谣中伤的闲话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was fired for spreading slanderous gossip about his own manager.  他因散布造谣中伤上司的闲话而被解雇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 traducing</del></td><td>/trəˈduːsɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>诽谤的，中伤的</td><td>traducing pamphlets<br>中伤性的小册子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traducing pamphlets circulated through the city days before the vote.  投票前几天，中伤性的小册子在全城流传。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vilifying</td><td>/ˈvɪləfaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>诬蔑的，肆意诽谤的</td><td>a vilifying editorial<br>肆意诽谤的社论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vilifying editorial cost the paper many readers.  那篇诽谤性社论让报纸失去许多读者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 adulatory</del></td><td>/ˈædʒələˌtɔri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谄媚的，过分吹捧的</td><td>an adulatory review<br>吹捧的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine ran an adulatory profile of the young director.  杂志刊登了一篇吹捧那位年轻导演的报道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 applauding</td><td>/əˈplɔːdɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>赞许的，鼓掌称赞的</td><td>an applauding audience<br>鼓掌称赞的观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She bowed twice to the applauding audience before leaving the stage.  她向鼓掌的观众鞠了两次躬才离开舞台。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 commendatory</td><td>/kəˈmendətɔːri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>赞许的，表扬的</td><td>a commendatory letter<br>一封表扬信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His file contains three commendatory letters from clients.  他的档案里有三封客户的表扬信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 eulogistic</del></td><td>/ˌjuːləˈdʒɪstɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>赞颂的，歌功颂德的</td><td>a eulogistic preface<br>颂扬式的前言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The biography is so eulogistic that it reads like an advertisement.  这本传记颂扬过度，读起来像广告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 eulogizing</td><td>/ˈjuːlədʒaɪzɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>颂扬的，称赞的</td><td>a eulogizing preface<br>颂扬式的序言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The eulogizing preface praised the author for ten pages.  那篇颂扬式序言用十页篇幅赞美作者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 laudatory</td><td>/ˈlɔːdətɔːri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>表示赞扬的</td><td>laudatory reviews<br>赞扬性的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel received laudatory reviews from critics who rarely praise debut authors.  这部小说获得了盛赞，而这些评论家很少称赞新人作家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 praising</td><td>/ˈpreɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>赞扬的，称赞的</td><td>praising words<br>赞扬的话语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach sent a short praising note to each player.  教练给每位球员发了一条简短的赞扬留言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lampoon</b></td><td>/læmˈpuːn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>讽刺</td><td>a savage political lampoon<br>犀利的政治讽刺作品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His savage political lampoon appeared on the front page of the weekly.  他犀利的政治讽刺作品登上了这份周刊的头版。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burlesque</td><td>/bɜːrˈlesk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>滑稽夸张的模仿讽刺作品；恶搞</td><td>a burlesque of politics<br>对政治的滑稽恶搞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The play is a clever burlesque of modern political debate.  这部戏是对当代政治辩论的巧妙滑稽模仿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 caricature</td><td>/ˈkærɪkətʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>讽刺画，夸张的滑稽模仿</td><td>draw a caricature<br>画一幅讽刺画</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cartoonist drew a cruel caricature of the new mayor.  漫画家画了一幅刻薄的新市长讽刺画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 farce</td><td>/fɑːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>闹剧；滑稽戏；荒唐事</td><td>turn into a farce<br>变成一场闹剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trial soon turned into a farce that embarrassed the whole court.  这场审判很快变成一场闹剧，让整个法庭都很难堪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mockery</td><td>/ˈmɑːkəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>笑柄；嘲弄，拙劣的模仿</td><td>make a mockery of justice<br>使正义成为笑柄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rushed trial made a mockery of justice in the eyes of observers.  在观察者看来，这场草率的审判使正义成了笑柄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parody</td><td>/ˈpærədi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（以嘲笑原作作者的）模仿作品，恶搞</td><td>a parody of the film<br>对该片的恶搞模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students wrote a hilarious parody of the morning speech at assembly.  学生们写了一篇滑稽的作品，恶搞集会上的早间讲话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ridicule</td><td>/ˈrɪdɪkjuːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>嘲笑；讥讽；愚弄</td><td>an object of ridicule<br>被嘲笑的对象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Classmates began to ridicule him for his old shoes and worn coat.  同学们开始因他的旧鞋和破外套而嘲笑他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spoof</td><td>/spuːf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>轻松幽默的模仿，小恶搞</td><td>a spoof of the news<br>对新闻的幽默模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The show aired a clever spoof of the evening news broadcast.  节目播出了对晚间新闻的一段巧妙恶搞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 travesty</td><td>/ˈtrævəsti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>拙劣的、嘲弄性模仿</td><td>a travesty of justice<br>对正义的拙劣讽刺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics called the hasty trial a travesty of justice.  批评者称这场匆忙的审判是对正义的拙劣讽刺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 eulogy</td><td>/ˈjuːlədʒi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>悼词；颂词，赞辞</td><td>deliver a moving eulogy<br>致感人的悼词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She delivered a moving eulogy at her grandfather's funeral.  她在祖父的葬礼上致了感人的悼词。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ode</td><td>/ˈoʊd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>颂歌，颂诗</td><td>an ode to autumn<br>一首秋之颂歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet wrote an ode celebrating the river of his childhood.  诗人写了一首颂诗，赞美童年的那条河。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 paean</td><td>/ˈpiːən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>欢乐颂，赞扬</td><td>a paean to freedom<br>对自由的赞歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The review read like a paean to the young violinist and her talent.  那篇评论读来像是对这位年轻小提琴手及其才华的赞歌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>languid</b></td><td>/ˈlæŋɡwɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没精打采的，虚弱的</td><td>a languid summer afternoon<br>慢悠悠的夏日午后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A languid summer afternoon passed with nobody willing to leave the shade.  一个慢悠悠的夏日午后过去了，没人愿意离开树荫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debilitated</td><td>/dəˈbɪləˌteɪtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的，衰弱的</td><td>debilitated by long illness<br>因久病而衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patient looked pale and debilitated after months of treatment.  经过数月治疗，病人显得苍白虚弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 effete</td><td>/ɪˈfiːt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的，衰落的</td><td>an effete aristocracy<br>衰落的贵族阶层</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An effete aristocracy clung to its titles long after the wealth had vanished.  衰落的贵族阶层在财富早已消失后仍死守头衔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enervated</td><td>/ˈɛnərveɪtəd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的，无力的</td><td>enervated by the heat<br>被暑热弄得虚弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long illness left him enervated and unusually pale.  长期患病使他虚弱且异常苍白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feeble</td><td>/ˈfiːbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的</td><td>a feeble voice<br>微弱的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old man answered in a feeble voice from the bed.  老人从床上用微弱的声音回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frail</td><td>/freɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的；脆弱的</td><td>a frail old woman<br>体弱的老妇人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A frail old woman waited alone at the crowded bus stop.  一位虚弱的老妇人独自等在拥挤的公交站。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infirm</td><td>/ɪnˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的；不果断的，优柔寡断的</td><td>old and infirm<br>年老体弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her grandmother grew too infirm to climb the stairs without help.  她的祖母身体太虚弱，没人帮忙就爬不了楼梯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lackadaisical</td><td>/ˌlækəˈdeɪzɪkəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>懒散的，无兴趣的</td><td>a lackadaisical attitude<br>懒散的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lackadaisical attitude toward homework worried both parents.  他对作业懒散的态度让父母都很担心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 spiritless</del></td><td>/ˈspɪrɪtləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无精打采的，没有生气的</td><td>a spiritless performance<br>毫无生气的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team gave a spiritless performance in the second half.  球队在下半场表现得毫无生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sapped</td><td>/ˈsæpt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>力气被耗尽的，虚弱的</td><td>sapped of energy<br>精力被耗尽</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team looked sapped after three games in one week.  一周三场比赛后球队看来精力耗尽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enfeebled</td><td>/enˈfiːbəld/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的，虚弱的</td><td>enfeebled by illness<br>因病而衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Enfeebled by fever, he could barely sit up.  因发烧而虚弱，他几乎坐不起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 animated</td><td>/ˈænəˌmeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活跃的，生气勃勃的</td><td>an animated discussion<br>一场热烈的讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The class turned into an animated debate about ethics.  这堂课变成了一场关于伦理的热烈辩论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 energetic</td><td>/ˌenərˈdʒetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的</td><td>an energetic performance<br>活力四射的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even at seventy she remains remarkably energetic and full of plans.  即使七十岁了，她依然精力充沛，计划满满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vehement</td><td>/ˈviːəmənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（情感）强烈的，热情的</td><td>vehement opposition<br>强烈的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made a vehement protest against the new tax.  她对新税提出了强烈的抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vivacious</td><td>/vɪˈveɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活泼的，快活的</td><td>a vivacious personality<br>活泼的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her vivacious manner made every stranger feel welcome at the table.  她活泼的举止让每个陌生人在餐桌旁都感到受欢迎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>languish</b></td><td>/ˈlæŋɡwɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>变得衰弱</td><td>languish in a damp cell<br>在潮湿牢房中日渐衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prisoners languished in damp cells for months without proper food.  囚犯们在潮湿的牢房里日渐衰弱，数月没有适当的食物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decay</td><td>/dɪˈkeɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>衰败，腐烂</td><td>fall into slow decay<br>逐渐衰败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The abandoned mill has been left to decay for decades.  这座废弃磨坊已任其衰败数十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 droop</td><td>/ˈdruːp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低垂，萎靡</td><td>the flowers begin to droop<br>花开始低垂萎蔫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plants drooped badly after three days without water.  三天没浇水，植物都严重萎蔫低垂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 emaciate</del></td><td>/ɪˈmeɪʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使消瘦，使憔悴</td><td>emaciate the patient's body<br>使病人身体消瘦衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months of fever had emaciated the patient's body almost beyond recognition.  数月高烧使病人的身体消瘦衰弱得几乎认不出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fade</td><td>/ˈfeɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>衰退，消退；褪色</td><td>hopes began to fade<br>希望开始消退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their hopes of rescue began to fade after three days.  三天后他们获救的希望开始消退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fail</td><td>/ˈfeɪl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>衰退，衰竭；失败</td><td>his strength failed<br>他体力衰竭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His eyesight began to fail in his early seventies.  他七十出头时视力开始衰退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flag</td><td>/flæɡ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>变得衰弱</td><td>his energy began to flag<br>他的精力开始衰减</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His energy began to flag after the twentieth mile.  跑到第二十英里后他的精力开始衰减。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sag</td><td>/ˈsæɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>下垂，下陷；衰退</td><td>the old roof sags badly<br>旧屋顶严重下陷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mattress sagged in the middle after years of use.  用了多年后，床垫中间下陷了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wither</td><td>/ˈwɪðər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>枯萎，凋谢</td><td>wither in the heat<br>在高温中枯萎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tomato plants withered after a week without rain or shade.  一周无雨又没有遮荫，番茄苗都枯萎了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 burgeon</td><td>/ˈbɜːrdʒən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>迅速成长扩大，蓬勃发展</td><td>a burgeoning market<br>迅速发展的市场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city's tech sector burgeoned after the new university opened.  新大学开办后，该市的科技产业迅速壮大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flourish</td><td>/ˈflɜːrɪʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>茂盛；繁荣</td><td>flourish in warm climates<br>在温暖气候中繁茂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Olive trees flourish in the dry, sunny hills of southern Spain.  橄榄树在西班牙南部干燥向阳的山丘上长得茂盛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 thrive</td><td>/ˈθraɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>兴旺，繁荣，茁壮成长</td><td>thrive in adversity<br>在逆境中兴旺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small shops thrive when the local economy is healthy.  当地经济健康时，小店就会兴旺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prosper</td><td>/ˈprɑːspər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>繁荣，兴旺，成功</td><td>prosper under new management<br>在新管理下兴旺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small bakery prospered once tourists discovered the town.  游客发现这个小镇后，那家小面包店就兴旺起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>languor</b></td><td>/ˈlæŋɡər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>懒惰；衰弱</td><td>summer languor<br>夏日的倦怠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heavy heat brought a languor that made work nearly impossible.  酷热带来一种倦怠，几乎使人无法工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 collapse</td><td>/kəˈlæps/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>失败</td><td>the collapse of talks<br>谈判的破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The collapse of the talks surprised nobody in the capital.  谈判的破裂在首都没让任何人感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exhaustion</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːstʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>极度疲劳；耗尽</td><td>collapse from exhaustion<br>因极度疲劳而倒下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rescuers worked until exhaustion forced them to rest.  救援人员一直工作到疲劳迫使他们休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frazzle</td><td>/ˈfræzəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>疲惫不堪，精疲力竭</td><td>worn to a frazzle<br>累得精疲力竭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Three night shifts wore her to a frazzle.  三个夜班让她累得精疲力竭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lassitude</td><td>/ˈlæsɪtuːd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>疲乏，无精打采</td><td>a feeling of lassitude<br>一种倦怠感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strange lassitude kept him in bed all afternoon.  一种奇怪的倦怠让他整个下午都躺在床上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 listlessness</td><td>/ˈlɪstləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倦怠，无精打采</td><td>an air of listlessness<br>一副无精打采的样子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strange listlessness settled over the office in August.  八月里办公室弥漫着一种奇怪的倦怠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stupor</td><td>/ˈstuːpər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迟钝，麻痹，无知觉</td><td>fall into a stupor<br>陷入麻木状态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He fell into a stupor and did not respond to our questions.  他陷入麻木状态，对我们的问题毫无反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torpor</td><td>/ˈtɔːrpər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>缺乏兴趣；（肉体或精神上的）迟钝，懒散</td><td>sink into torpor<br>陷入迟钝麻木</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat sank the whole village into a heavy afternoon torpor.  暑热让整个村子陷入沉沉的午后麻木。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prostration</td><td>/prɑːˈstreɪʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>极度衰弱，衰竭</td><td>heat prostration<br>中暑虚脱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two hikers were treated for heat prostration at the trailhead.  两名徒步者在步道口接受了中暑虚脱的治疗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debilitation</td><td>/dɪˌbɪlɪˈteɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>虚弱，衰弱</td><td>gradual debilitation<br>逐渐的衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The disease causes slow debilitation of the muscles.  这种疾病导致肌肉缓慢衰弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enervation</del></td><td>/ˌenərˈveɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>虚弱无力，元气丧失</td><td>a sense of enervation<br>一种虚弱无力感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Humid August afternoons brought a familiar enervation that no coffee cured.  八月潮湿的午后带来熟悉的虚弱无力感，喝咖啡也无济于事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enfeeblement</del></td><td>/ɪnˈfiːbəlmənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>衰弱，削弱</td><td>the enfeeblement of the state<br>国家力量的衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians debate the causes of the empire's enfeeblement.  史学家争论这个帝国衰弱的原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fragility</td><td>/frəˈdʒɪləti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>脆弱，虚弱</td><td>the fragility of the truce<br>休战的脆弱性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crisis exposed the fragility of the banking system.  这场危机暴露了银行体系的脆弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infirmity</td><td>/ɪnˈfɜːrmɪti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>体弱，疾病</td><td>the infirmities of age<br>老年的体弱多病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Despite growing infirmity, she kept teaching until eighty.  尽管日渐体弱，她一直教到八十岁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 verve</td><td>/vɜːrv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，热情</td><td>play with verve<br>充满激情地演奏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist played that difficult passage with remarkable verve.  这位年轻钢琴家以非凡的热情演奏了那段高难度乐章。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vim</td><td>/vɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，精力</td><td>full of vim and vigor<br>精力充沛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At seventy he still worked with surprising vim and good humor.  七十岁的他干活仍精力惊人，心情愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 animation</td><td>/ˌænəˈmeɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>生气，活力</td><td>speak with animation<br>兴致勃勃地讲话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She described the trip with unusual animation and plenty of gestures.  她异常兴致勃勃地描述了那次旅行，还比划了许多手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vitality</td><td>/vaɪˈtæləti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，生命力</td><td>brim with vitality<br>充满活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old market still brims with vitality on early summer mornings.  夏日清晨，这座老市场依然充满活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 robustness</td><td>/roʊˈbʌstnəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>强健，稳健</td><td>the robustness of the design<br>设计的稳健性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers praised the robustness of the old bridge.  工程师赞赏这座旧桥的稳固。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 strength</td><td>/ˈstreŋkθ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>力量，强度</td><td>regain one's strength<br>恢复体力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took him months to regain his strength.  他花了几个月才恢复体力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vivacity</td><td>/vɪˈvæsəti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活泼，快活</td><td>youthful vivacity<br>年轻人的活泼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her vivacity lit up an otherwise dull dinner.  她的活泼点亮了原本乏味的晚宴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lank</b></td><td>/læŋk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>细长瘦弱的；不僵硬的，柔软松弛的</td><td>lank hair<br>柔软下垂的头发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lank hair fell over his forehead whenever he leaned forward.  他柔软下垂的头发每次前倾时都垂到额前。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 emaciated</td><td>/ɪˈmeɪʃieɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>极瘦的，消瘦的</td><td>an emaciated stray dog<br>一只瘦骨嶙峋的流浪狗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rescued horses were painfully emaciated but still alive.  被救的马匹瘦得让人心痛，但还活着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lean</td><td>/ˈliːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>瘦的，精瘦的</td><td>lean and muscular<br>精瘦而结实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of running left him lean and quick.  多年跑步让他精瘦而敏捷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slender</td><td>/ˈslendər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>苗条的，细长的</td><td>a slender waist<br>纤细的腰身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She held the flower by its slender stem.  她捏着那朵花细长的茎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 svelte</td><td>/svelt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（女人）体态苗条的，优雅的</td><td>a svelte silhouette<br>苗条优雅的身影</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her svelte silhouette drew admiring glances across the crowded ballroom.  她苗条优雅的身影在拥挤的舞厅里引来赞赏的目光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tenuous</td><td>/ˈtenjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>站不住脚的，脆弱的；纤细的</td><td>a tenuous argument<br>站不住脚的论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer's case rested on a tenuous argument about intent.  律师的诉讼建立在关于动机的站不住脚的论点上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thin</td><td>/ˈθɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>使变稀；薄的，瘦的</td><td>thin the paint slightly<br>把颜料稍微稀释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thin the sauce with a little warm water before serving.  上菜前用少量温水把酱汁调稀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
