<tr><td>↳近 droopy</td><td>/ˈdruːpi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>下垂的，松垂的</td><td>droopy eyelids<br>下垂的眼皮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog's droopy ears made it look permanently sad.  这只狗下垂的耳朵让它看起来总是很忧伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flaccid</td><td>/ˈflæsɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不结实的，松弛的</td><td>flaccid muscles<br>松弛的肌肉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weeks in bed left his leg muscles flaccid and weak.  数周卧床使他的腿部肌肉松弛无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 floppy</td><td>/ˈflɑːpi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>松软的，松垂的</td><td>a floppy hat<br>软塌塌的帽子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She wore a floppy straw hat to block the sun.  她戴着一顶软塌塌的草帽遮阳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 yielding</td><td>/ˈjiːldɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，易屈服的</td><td>a yielding personality<br>顺从的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her yielding nature made her an easy target for pushy salesmen.  她顺从的性格使她很容易被强势的推销员盯上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fat</td><td>/ˈfæt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>肥胖的，多脂的</td><td>a fat tabby cat<br>一只肥胖的花斑猫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fat cat refused to move off the warm step.  那只肥猫拒绝离开温暖的台阶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fleshy</td><td>/ˈfleʃi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>多肉的，肥胖的</td><td>fleshy arms<br>多肉的手臂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He had a broad, fleshy face and kind eyes.  他有一张宽阔多肉的脸和一双慈祥的眼睛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gross</td><td>/ˈɡroʊs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过于肥胖的；粗俗的</td><td>a gross, bloated figure<br>臃肿肥胖的身形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The caricature gave the mayor a gross, swollen belly.  那幅漫画给市长画了个臃肿肥大的肚子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obese</td><td>/oʊˈbiːs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肥胖的，过度肥胖的</td><td>clinically obese<br>临床上属肥胖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic treats children who are clinically obese.  这家诊所治疗临床肥胖的儿童。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inflexible</td><td>/ˌɪnˈfleksəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不肯变通的，僵化的</td><td>an inflexible rule<br>不容变通的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager was inflexible about the submission deadline.  经理在提交截止日期上毫不通融。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rigid</td><td>/ˈrɪdʒəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，僵硬的</td><td>rigid discipline<br>严格的纪律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school enforces rigid rules about mobile phones.  学校对手机执行严格的规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stiff</td><td>/stɪf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>僵硬的，无法弯曲的；艰苦的，费力的</td><td>a stiff leather collar<br>硬挺的皮质衣领</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stiff leather collar rubbed his neck raw during the long march.  长途行军中，硬挺的皮领把他的脖子磨破了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sturdy</td><td>/ˈstɜːrdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强健的，结实的</td><td>a sturdy wooden table<br>结实的木桌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sturdy wooden table stood in the middle of the farmhouse kitchen.  农舍厨房中间摆着一张结实的木桌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tense</td><td>/ˈtens/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>紧绷的，紧张的</td><td>tense shoulders<br>紧绷的肩膀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His tense jaw showed how hard he was concentrating.  他紧绷的下颌显示出他多么专注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lapse</b></td><td>/læps/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>小过失</td><td>a lapse of memory<br>记忆上的小失误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brief lapse of memory made him forget his colleague's name entirely.  一时的记忆失误使他完全忘了同事的名字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blunder</td><td>/ˈblʌndər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>大错，失误；犯大错</td><td>a costly blunder<br>代价高昂的失误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sending the letter unsigned was a costly blunder.  把信未签名就寄出是个代价高昂的失误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fumble</td><td>/ˈfʌmbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>笨拙地做</td><td>fumble for the keys<br>笨拙地摸找钥匙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He fumbled for the keys in his dark coat pocket.  他在昏暗的外套口袋里笨拙地摸找钥匙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gaffe</td><td>/ɡæf/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（社交上）失礼，失态</td><td>a diplomatic gaffe<br>外交失礼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His remark about the queen was a serious diplomatic gaffe.  他关于女王的言论是严重的外交失礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 miscue</td><td>/ˈmɪsˌkjuː/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>失误，差错</td><td>an embarrassing miscue<br>一次尴尬的失误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single miscue in the relay cost them the medal.  接力中的一次失误让他们丢了奖牌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 oversight</td><td>/ˈoʊvərsaɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>疏忽，失察；监管</td><td>due to a clerical oversight<br>因文书疏忽</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your name was omitted through a simple clerical oversight.  你的名字因一个小小的文书疏忽被遗漏了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 peccadillo</del></td><td>/ˌpekəˈdɪloʊ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>小过失，小毛病</td><td>overlook his peccadilloes<br>忽略他的小过失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters forgave the mayor's peccadilloes because of his real achievements.  因为确有实绩，选民原谅了市长的小过失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>largesse</b></td><td>/lɑːrˈdʒes/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>慷慨</td><td>royal largesse<br>王室的慷慨赏赐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum was built entirely through the largesse of one wealthy family.  这座博物馆完全是靠一个富有家族的慷慨捐赠建成的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 bountifulness</del></td><td>/ˈbaʊntɪfəlnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>慷慨，丰裕</td><td>the bountifulness of the harvest<br>收成的丰裕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors still speak of her bountifulness at holidays.  邻居们仍谈起她节日里的慷慨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 generosity</td><td>/ˌdʒenəˈrɑːsəti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>慷慨，大方；宽厚</td><td>act of great generosity<br>极为慷慨的举动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library was built through the generosity of one donor.  这座图书馆靠一位捐赠者的慷慨建成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 munificence</td><td>/mjuːˈnɪfɪsəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>慷慨，大方</td><td>the donor's munificence<br>捐赠者的慷慨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library exists thanks to one man's munificence.  这座图书馆的存在全靠一个人的慷慨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 openhandedness</del></td><td>/ˌoʊpənˈhændɪdnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>慷慨大方</td><td>legendary openhandedness<br>传奇般的慷慨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His openhandedness toward struggling students was legendary in the department.  他对困难学生的慷慨在系里是出了名的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 philanthropy</td><td>/fɪˈlænθrəpi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>慈善，博爱</td><td>corporate philanthropy<br>企业慈善事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She turned to philanthropy after selling the company.  卖掉公司后她转向慈善事业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 miserliness</del></td><td>/ˈmaɪzərlinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>吝啬，小气</td><td>notorious miserliness<br>出名的吝啬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His miserliness left the heirs a fortune and no memories.  他的吝啬留给继承人一笔财富却没有回忆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 parsimony</td><td>/ˈpɑːrsəˌmoʊni/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>吝啬，极度节俭</td><td>fiscal parsimony<br>财政上的极度节俭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Decades of parsimony left the museum without a roof fund.  数十年的过度节俭让博物馆没有修屋顶的钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 penury</td><td>/ˈpenjəri/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>贫穷；吝啬，节俭</td><td>live in penury<br>生活贫困</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The once wealthy family ended its days in complete penury.  这个曾经富裕的家庭最终在彻底的贫困中度日。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stinginess</td><td>/ˈstɪndʒinɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>吝啬，小气</td><td>shocking stinginess<br>令人吃惊的吝啬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The waiter complained loudly about the large party's shocking stinginess.  服务员大声抱怨那桌大客人惊人的吝啬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lash</b></td><td>/læʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>猛击，撞击</td><td>rain lashing the windows<br>雨点猛打窗户</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain lashed the windows all night and kept the children awake.  大雨整夜猛打窗户，让孩子们无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baste</td><td>/ˈbeɪst/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>痛打；严厉批评</td><td>baste him with criticism<br>用批评痛击他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The columnist basted the mayor for wasting so much public money.  专栏作家痛批市长浪费了大量公款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hammer</td><td>/ˈhæmər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>屡次重申</td><td>hammer home the point<br>反复强调这一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach hammered home the importance of defense at every single practice.  教练在每一次训练中都反复强调防守的重要性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lace</td><td>/ˈleɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>猛击；系带</td><td>lace into an opponent<br>猛烈攻击对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boxer laced into his rival in the final round.  拳手在最后一轮猛击对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lambaste</td><td>/læmˈbeɪst/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉斥责，痛骂</td><td>lambaste the government<br>痛斥政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lambasted the film for its shallow, sentimental ending.  批评家严厉斥责这部电影结局浅薄而煽情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 punch</td><td>/ˈpʌntʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>用拳猛击</td><td>punch a hole in it<br>在上面打个洞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He punched the wall and broke two knuckles.  他猛击墙壁，弄断了两根指骨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lassitude</b></td><td>/ˈlæsɪtuːd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>疲乏，无精打采</td><td>a feeling of lassitude<br>一种倦怠感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strange lassitude kept him in bed all afternoon.  一种奇怪的倦怠让他整个下午都躺在床上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 collapse</td><td>/kəˈlæps/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>失败</td><td>the collapse of talks<br>谈判的破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The collapse of the talks surprised nobody in the capital.  谈判的破裂在首都没让任何人感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exhaustion</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːstʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>极度疲劳；耗尽</td><td>collapse from exhaustion<br>因极度疲劳而倒下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rescuers worked until exhaustion forced them to rest.  救援人员一直工作到疲劳迫使他们休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frazzle</td><td>/ˈfræzəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>疲惫不堪，精疲力竭</td><td>worn to a frazzle<br>累得精疲力竭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Three night shifts wore her to a frazzle.  三个夜班让她累得精疲力竭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 languor</td><td>/ˈlæŋɡər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>懒惰；衰弱</td><td>summer languor<br>夏日的倦怠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heavy heat brought a languor that made work nearly impossible.  酷热带来一种倦怠，几乎使人无法工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 listlessness</td><td>/ˈlɪstləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倦怠，无精打采</td><td>an air of listlessness<br>一副无精打采的样子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strange listlessness settled over the office in August.  八月里办公室弥漫着一种奇怪的倦怠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stupor</td><td>/ˈstuːpər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迟钝，麻痹，无知觉</td><td>fall into a stupor<br>陷入麻木状态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He fell into a stupor and did not respond to our questions.  他陷入麻木状态，对我们的问题毫无反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torpor</td><td>/ˈtɔːrpər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>缺乏兴趣；（肉体或精神上的）迟钝，懒散</td><td>sink into torpor<br>陷入迟钝麻木</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat sank the whole village into a heavy afternoon torpor.  暑热让整个村子陷入沉沉的午后麻木。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prostration</td><td>/prɑːˈstreɪʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>极度衰弱，衰竭</td><td>heat prostration<br>中暑虚脱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two hikers were treated for heat prostration at the trailhead.  两名徒步者在步道口接受了中暑虚脱的治疗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 verve</td><td>/vɜːrv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，热情</td><td>play with verve<br>充满激情地演奏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist played that difficult passage with remarkable verve.  这位年轻钢琴家以非凡的热情演奏了那段高难度乐章。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vim</td><td>/vɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，精力</td><td>full of vim and vigor<br>精力充沛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At seventy he still worked with surprising vim and good humor.  七十岁的他干活仍精力惊人，心情愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 animation</td><td>/ˌænəˈmeɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>生气，活力</td><td>speak with animation<br>兴致勃勃地讲话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She described the trip with unusual animation and plenty of gestures.  她异常兴致勃勃地描述了那次旅行，还比划了许多手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vitality</td><td>/vaɪˈtæləti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，生命力</td><td>brim with vitality<br>充满活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old market still brims with vitality on early summer mornings.  夏日清晨，这座老市场依然充满活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>latent</b></td><td>/ˈleɪtnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>潜在的，不活跃的</td><td>latent talent<br>潜在的才能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good teacher can discover latent talent in the quietest student.  好老师能在最安静的学生身上发现潜在的才能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dormant</td><td>/ˈdɔːrmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>静止的，不活跃的</td><td>a dormant volcano<br>休眠火山</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The virus can stay dormant in the body for years.  这种病毒可在体内潜伏多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fallow</td><td>/ˈfæloʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>休耕的</td><td>leave the field fallow<br>让田地休耕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farmer left the tired field fallow for a whole season.  农民让这块贫瘠的田地整季休耕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inert</td><td>/ɪˈnɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（人）懒惰缺乏活力的；（物品）惰性的</td><td>an inert gas<br>惰性气体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Helium is an inert gas, so it rarely reacts with other elements.  氦是一种惰性气体，因此很少与其他元素发生反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inoperative</td><td>/ɪnˈɑːpərətɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不起作用的，失效的</td><td>an inoperative rule<br>已失效的规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old safety clause in the contract is now inoperative.  合同里那条旧的安全条款现已失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 apparent</td><td>/əˈperənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的；表面上的</td><td>for no apparent reason<br>没有明显原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It soon became apparent that the machine had never worked.  很快就明显看出这台机器从未运转过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 evident</td><td>/ˈevɪdənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，显然的</td><td>clearly evident<br>显而易见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her talent for music was evident from a very early age.  她的音乐才华从很小就显而易见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 manifest</td><td>/ˈmænɪfest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>显然的，明显易懂的；显现，显露</td><td>manifest injustice<br>明显的不公</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His discomfort at the question was manifest to everyone seated around the table.  他对这个问题的不适让围坐桌边的每个人都看得明明白白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obvious</td><td>/ˈɑːbviəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，显而易见的</td><td>an obvious mistake<br>明显的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The answer seemed obvious once she explained the method.  她解释了方法后，答案似乎显而易见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plain</td><td>/ˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，十足的；朴素的，清楚的</td><td>plain stupid<br>简直愚蠢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning was just plain stupid.  忽视警告纯粹是愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>latitude</b></td><td>/ˈlætɪtuːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>（行动或言论）自由</td><td>allow considerable latitude<br>给予相当大的自由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students are allowed considerable latitude in choosing their own elective courses.  学生在选择选修课时被给予相当大的自由度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 authorization</td><td>/ˌɔːθərəˈzeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>授权，批准</td><td>official authorization<br>正式授权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one may enter the lab without written authorization.  未经书面授权，任何人不得进入实验室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 license</td><td>/ˈlaɪsns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>许可，认可；自由</td><td>a license to practice<br>执业许可证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She finally obtained a license to practice medicine in three different states.  她终于取得了在三个不同州行医的执业许可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 freedom</td><td>/ˈfriːdəm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>自由，自主权</td><td>freedom of choice<br>选择的自由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Staff enjoy real freedom in setting their own hours.  员工在安排工作时间上有真正的自由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 leeway</td><td>/ˈliːˌweɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>余地，自由度</td><td>give someone leeway<br>给某人一些余地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new contract gives designers plenty of leeway on materials.  新合同在材料方面给设计师留了很大余地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 free hand</td><td>/friː ˈhænd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>全权，自由行动权</td><td>give a free hand<br>给予全权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board gave her a free hand to rebuild the team.  董事会授予她全权重建团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 limitation</td><td>/ˌlɪmɪˈteɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>限制，局限</td><td>impose limitations on<br>对…施加限制</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Budget limitations forced the crew to shoot indoors.  预算限制迫使剧组在室内拍摄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>laudatory</b></td><td>/ˈlɔːdətɔːri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>表示赞扬的</td><td>laudatory reviews<br>赞扬性的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel received laudatory reviews from critics who rarely praise debut authors.  这部小说获得了盛赞，而这些评论家很少称赞新人作家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 adulatory</del></td><td>/ˈædʒələˌtɔri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谄媚的，过分吹捧的</td><td>an adulatory review<br>吹捧的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine ran an adulatory profile of the young director.  杂志刊登了一篇吹捧那位年轻导演的报道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commendatory</td><td>/kəˈmendətɔːri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>赞许的，表扬的</td><td>a commendatory letter<br>一封表扬信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His file contains three commendatory letters from clients.  他的档案里有三封客户的表扬信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complimentary</td><td>/ˌkɑːmpləˈmentəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>免费赠送的；赞美的</td><td>a complimentary breakfast<br>免费的早餐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hotel offers a complimentary breakfast to every overnight guest.  酒店为每位住宿客人提供免费早餐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extolling</td><td>/ɪkˈstoʊlɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>赞颂，极力称赞</td><td>extolling his many virtues<br>极力称赞他的优点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The article spent pages extolling the benefits of daily exercise.  文章用数页篇幅盛赞每日锻炼的益处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 eulogistic</del></td><td>/ˌjuːləˈdʒɪstɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>赞颂的，歌功颂德的</td><td>a eulogistic preface<br>颂扬式的前言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The biography is so eulogistic that it reads like an advertisement.  这本传记颂扬过度，读起来像广告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 panegyric</td><td>/ˌpænəˈdʒɪrɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>赞颂之词，颂文</td><td>deliver a long panegyric<br>发表一篇长颂词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The retirement dinner ended with a long panegyric to the old professor.  退休晚宴以对这位老教授的长篇颂词结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 derogatory</td><td>/dɪˈrɑːɡətɔːri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贬损的，轻蔑的</td><td>derogatory language<br>贬损性的言辞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He apologized for the derogatory term he used.  他为使用那个贬损词语道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 depreciatory</del></td><td>/dɪˈpriːʃətɔːri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贬低的，轻视的</td><td>a depreciatory tone<br>轻视的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke of her own paintings in depreciatory terms.  她用自贬的措辞谈论自己的画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disparaging</td><td>/dɪˈsperɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贬低的，轻蔑的</td><td>disparaging remarks<br>贬低的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Disparaging remarks about volunteers upset the staff.  贬低志愿者的言论让员工不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pejorative</td><td>/pɪˈdʒɔːrətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻蔑的，贬低的</td><td>a pejorative label<br>带贬义的标签</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics used the word in a purely pejorative sense.  评论家用这个词纯粹是取其贬义。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lavish</b></td><td>/ˈlævɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>奢侈的，大量的，无节制的；挥霍，浪费</td><td>a lavish banquet<br>奢华的宴会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They hosted a lavish banquet for three hundred guests in the garden.  他们在花园里为三百位客人举办了一场奢华的宴会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 copious</td><td>/ˈkoʊpiəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，丰富的</td><td>take copious notes<br>做大量笔记</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took copious notes during every lecture and reread them each night.  她每堂课都记大量笔记，并在每晚重读。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exuberant</td><td>/ɪɡˈzuːbərənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情洋溢的；繁茂的</td><td>exuberant applause from fans<br>粉丝热情的掌声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Exuberant applause from fans greeted the singer's surprise return.  粉丝们热情洋溢的掌声迎接歌手的意外回归。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gushing</td><td>/ˈɡʌʃɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>喷涌的；过分热情的</td><td>a gushing spring<br>喷涌的泉水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A gushing spring supplies the whole village with water.  一股喷涌的泉水供给全村用水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lush</td><td>/lʌʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>茂盛的，繁茂的</td><td>lush vegetation<br>茂盛的植被</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lush vegetation covered the valley floor after weeks of steady rain.  连续数周的降雨之后，茂盛的植被覆盖了谷底。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 luxuriant</td><td>/lʌɡˈʒʊriənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>茂盛的，繁茂的</td><td>luxuriant vegetation<br>茂盛的植被</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Luxuriant ferns covered the damp floor of the forest.  茂盛的蕨类覆盖了潮湿的林地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 opulent</td><td>/ˈɑːpjələnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>富裕的；华丽的</td><td>an opulent hotel lobby<br>豪华的酒店大厅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The opulent ballroom was lined with gold mirrors and velvet chairs.  这间华丽的舞厅摆满了金框镜子和天鹅绒椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profuse</td><td>/prəˈfjuːs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，丰富的</td><td>profuse apologies<br>再三的道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He offered profuse apologies for arriving so late.  他为迟到再三道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 riotous</td><td>/ˈraɪətəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>繁茂的；喧闹的</td><td>a riotous display of color<br>缤纷繁茂的色彩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The garden was a riotous mass of summer flowers.  花园里夏花繁茂缤纷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blow</td><td>/ˈbloʊ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>挥霍，乱花钱</td><td>blow one's savings<br>挥霍掉积蓄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He will blow his entire bonus on a week in Tokyo.  他会把整笔奖金挥霍在东京的一周上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dissipate</td><td>/ˈdɪsɪpeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>驱散，消散；浪费</td><td>dissipate the fog<br>驱散雾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The morning mist dissipated as soon as the sun rose.  太阳一升起，晨雾就消散了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fritter</td><td>/ˈfrɪtər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>浪费，挥霍</td><td>fritter away the afternoon<br>把下午浪费掉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She frittered away the whole grant on minor equipment.  她把整笔资助挥霍在零碎设备上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 misspend</del></td><td>/mɪsˈspend/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>滥用，浪费</td><td>misspend public funds<br>滥用公共资金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The audit showed the agency had misspent millions.  审计显示该机构浪费了数百万。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squander</td><td>/ˈskwɑːndər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>浪费</td><td>squander an opportunity<br>浪费机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not squander this opportunity to study abroad for a year.  不要浪费这次出国留学一年的机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 waste</td><td>/ˈweɪst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词、动词</td><td>荒芜的；浪费；废物</td><td>waste land<br>荒地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing grows on this waste ground beside the old factory.  旧工厂旁的这片荒地上什么也长不出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 moderate</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>缓和，减轻；适度的</td><td>moderate one's demands<br>降低自己的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He agreed to moderate his tone during the debate.  他同意在辩论中缓和语气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 modest</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>谦虚的；不大的，适度的</td><td>modest about her success<br>对成功很谦虚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed modest about his award and thanked his team.  他对自己的奖项很谦虚，还感谢了团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reasonable</td><td>/ˈriːzənəbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>合理的，通情达理的</td><td>a reasonable request<br>合理的请求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That seems like a perfectly reasonable request.  那似乎是完全合理的要求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 temperate</td><td>/ˈtempərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（言行举止）有分寸的，有节制的</td><td>a temperate response<br>有分寸的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a temperate reply to a very rude question.  面对无礼的问题她的回答很有分寸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conserve</td><td>/kənˈsɜːrv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>节约，保存，保护</td><td>conserve energy<br>节约能源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We must conserve water during the long summer drought.  漫长的夏季干旱期间我们必须节约用水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>leaven</b></td><td>/ˈlevn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>在…中加入轻松、活泼或变更的因素</td><td>leaven a dry lecture<br>使枯燥讲座变生动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A few good jokes leavened an otherwise dry lecture on tax law.  几个好笑话让一场本来枯燥的税法讲座变得生动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imbue</td><td>/ɪmˈbjuː/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>灌输，使充满</td><td>imbue students with confidence<br>使学生充满自信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her teachers imbued her with a lasting love of poetry.  老师们使她对诗歌怀有持久的热爱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infuse</td><td>/ɪnˈfjuːz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>灌输，使…充满</td><td>infuse new energy into<br>注入新的活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new members infused fresh energy into the struggling drama club.  新成员为陷入困境的戏剧社注入了新的活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ingrain</td><td>/ɪnˈɡreɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使根深蒂固，深植</td><td>ingrain a sense of duty<br>把责任感深植于心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of training ingrained in her the habit of checking every figure twice.  多年训练使她养成了把每个数字复查两遍的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inoculate</td><td>/ɪˈnɑːkjəleɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>接种；灌输，注入</td><td>inoculate against measles<br>接种麻疹疫苗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every child here is inoculated before starting school.  这里每个孩子入学前都接种疫苗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inspire</td><td>/ɪnˈspaɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓舞，激励；引发</td><td>inspire confidence in others<br>让他人产生信心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her speech inspired the students to work much harder.  她的演讲激励学生更加努力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 permeate</td><td>/ˈpɜːrmieɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>弥漫，渗透</td><td>permeate the whole building<br>弥漫整栋楼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smell of fresh bread permeated the entire building by seven.  七点前，新鲜面包的香味弥漫了整栋楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 steep</td><td>/stiːp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>浸泡，浸渍</td><td>steep tea leaves in hot water<br>把茶叶泡在热水里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Steep the dried leaves in boiling water for about ten minutes.  把干叶子在沸水里浸泡约十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 suffuse</del></td><td>/səˈfjuːz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（色彩等）弥漫，染遍，充满</td><td>suffused with warm light<br>沐浴在暖光中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Warm evening light suffused the quiet room with a golden glow.  傍晚的暖光把安静的房间染成金色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extract</td><td>/ɪkˈstrækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>拔出，抽出；提取</td><td>extract a bad tooth<br>拔掉一颗坏牙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dentist had to extract a badly decayed back tooth.  牙医不得不拔掉一颗严重腐坏的后牙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>leer</b></td><td>/lɪr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>色迷迷地看，淫邪地注视</td><td>leer at a stranger<br>色迷迷地盯着陌生人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He leered at the stranger across the bar until she walked away.  他色迷迷地盯着酒吧对面的陌生人，直到她走开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squint</td><td>/ˈskwɪnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>斜视，眯眼看</td><td>squint into the sun<br>眯眼看向太阳</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He squinted at the tiny print on the label.  他眯着眼看标签上的小字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gape</td><td>/ˈɡeɪp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>目瞪口呆地看，张口凝视</td><td>gape in astonishment<br>惊讶地张口凝视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tourists gaped at the collapsed tower for hours.  游客们盯着倒塌的塔看了好几个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gaze</td><td>/ˈɡeɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>凝视，注视</td><td>gaze at the stars<br>凝望星空</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They gazed at the mountains until the sun had finally set.  他们凝望群山，直到太阳终于落下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glare</td><td>/ˈɡler/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>怒视，瞪眼</td><td>glare at the driver<br>怒视那个司机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She glared at him until he put the phone away.  她怒视着他，直到他收起手机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 goggle</td><td>/ˈɡɑːɡl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>凝视</td><td>goggle at the screen<br>凝视屏幕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tourists goggled at the enormous statue for several silent minutes.  游客们默默凝视那尊巨大的雕像好几分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stare</td><td>/ˈster/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>盯着看，凝视</td><td>stare in disbelief<br>难以置信地盯着</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stared at the letter for a long time without speaking.  他久久盯着那封信，一言不发。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>leery</b></td><td>/ˈlɪri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>怀疑的，不信任的</td><td>leery of strangers<br>对陌生人存有戒心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Having been cheated once, she remains leery of every online seller.  被骗过一次后，她对每个网上卖家都存有戒心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dubious</td><td>/ˈduːbiəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满不定性的，值得怀疑的</td><td>a dubious claim<br>可疑的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The advertisement made a dubious claim that no serious scientist would support.  那则广告作出了一个没有严肃科学家会支持的可疑说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distrustful</td><td>/dɪsˈtrʌstfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不信任的，怀疑的</td><td>distrustful of officials<br>不信任官员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters remain distrustful of every promise made.  选民对所做的每个承诺仍不信任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 skeptical</td><td>/ˈskeptəkəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>怀疑的，不轻信的</td><td>skeptical of claims<br>对说法持怀疑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters remain skeptical about promises made just before an election.  选民对选前才作出的承诺仍持怀疑态度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suspicious</td><td>/səˈspɪʃəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>怀疑的；可疑的</td><td>suspicious of his motives<br>怀疑他的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guard grew suspicious of the unmarked van.  保安对那辆无标识的货车起了疑心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wary</td><td>/ˈweri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>警惕的，谨慎的</td><td>wary of strangers<br>对陌生人警惕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children are taught to be wary of strangers offering free rides.  孩子们被教导要警惕主动提供搭车的陌生人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 credulous</td><td>/ˈkredʒələs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻信的，易受骗的</td><td>a credulous audience<br>轻信的听众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The advertisement was designed to fool credulous shoppers with fake reviews.  这则广告用虚假评论来欺骗轻信的购物者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>legacy</b></td><td>/ˈleɡəsi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>遗产</td><td>leave a lasting legacy<br>留下长久的遗产</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reformer left a lasting legacy of free schools across the province.  这位改革者在全省留下了免费学校这一长久的遗产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bequest</td><td>/bɪˈkwest/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>遗赠，遗产</td><td>a generous bequest<br>一笔丰厚的遗赠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hospital wing was funded by a single bequest.  这栋医院侧楼由一笔遗赠资助建成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heritage</td><td>/ˈherətədʒ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>遗产，传统</td><td>cultural heritage<br>文化遗产</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old town is protected as world heritage.  这座古城作为世界遗产受到保护。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 patrimony</td><td>/ˈpætrəˌmoʊni/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>继承的财产，祖产</td><td>squander one's patrimony<br>挥霍掉祖产</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sold the last of his patrimony to pay debts.  他卖掉最后的祖产来还债。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lucrative</b></td><td>/ˈluːkrətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有利可图的</td><td>a lucrative contract<br>一份有利可图的合同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Signing the lucrative contract changed the young athlete's life overnight.  签下这份有利可图的合同一夜之间改变了这位年轻运动员的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profitable</td><td>/ˈprɑːfətəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有利可图的；有益的</td><td>a profitable business<br>赚钱的生意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bakery finally became profitable in its third busy year.  这家面包店在忙碌的第三年终于开始赚钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remunerative</td><td>/rɪˈmjuːnərətɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有报酬的，赚钱的</td><td>remunerative employment<br>有报酬的工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Translation proved more remunerative than teaching.  翻译比教书更赚钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unprofitable</td><td>/ʌnˈprɑːfɪtəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无利可图的，徒劳的</td><td>an unprofitable branch<br>无利可图的分店</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They closed three unprofitable branches last spring.  去年春天他们关闭了三家无利可图的分店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>legion</b></td><td>/ˈliːdʒən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的</td><td>his admirers are legion<br>他的仰慕者众多</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His admirers are legion, though few of them have read his poems.  他的仰慕者众多，尽管其中很少有人读过他的诗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beaucoup</td><td>/ˌboʊˈkuː/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>许多的，大量的</td><td>beaucoup problems<br>许许多多的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old truck came with beaucoup problems.  这辆旧卡车带来了一大堆问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 multifold</del></td><td>/ˈmʌltɪfoʊld/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>多样的，众多的</td><td>multifold benefits<br>多方面的益处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reform brought multifold benefits to small farmers.  这项改革给小农带来多方面的益处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 multitudinous</td><td>/ˌmʌltɪˈtuːdɪnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>为数众多的</td><td>multitudinous stars<br>繁星无数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Multitudinous seabirds nested along the cliff.  数量众多的海鸟在崖壁上筑巢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 numerous</td><td>/ˈnuːmərəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>众多的，许多的</td><td>on numerous occasions<br>在许多场合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has warned us about this on numerous occasions.  她在许多场合就此警告过我们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 few</td><td>/ˈfjuː/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>很少的，少数的</td><td>very few visitors<br>极少的来访者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Few visitors reach the island in winter.  冬天很少有游客到达这座岛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lack in number</td><td>/læk ɪn ˈnʌmbər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>数量不足</td><td>lack in number and skill<br>数量与技能均不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defenders were lacking in number but still held the gate.  守军数量不足，却仍守住了城门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lenient</b></td><td>/ˈliːniənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽大仁慈的</td><td>a lenient sentence<br>从轻的判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge handed down a lenient sentence because of the boy's age.  由于这个男孩年纪小，法官作出了从轻的判决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clement</td><td>/ˈklemənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，善良的；气候温和的</td><td>a clement judge<br>宽容的法官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A clement judge gave the young offender a second chance.  一位宽容的法官给了这名年轻罪犯第二次机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gentle</td><td>/ˈdʒentəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温和的，轻柔的</td><td>a gentle voice<br>温柔的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave the door a gentle push and stepped inside.  他轻轻推了一下门，走了进去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 merciful</td><td>/ˈmɜːrsɪfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>仁慈的，宽厚的</td><td>a merciful judge<br>宽仁的法官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court was merciful and gave him only a small fine.  法庭很宽仁，只罚了他少许款项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mild</td><td>/ˈmaɪld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温和的，不严厉的</td><td>a mild punishment<br>轻微的处罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The winter here is unusually mild and rarely brings snow.  这里的冬天格外温和，很少下雪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sparing</td><td>/ˈsperɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>节俭的，节约的</td><td>sparing use of salt<br>节省地用盐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was sparing with salt because of his doctor's strict orders.  遵医嘱，他用盐十分节省。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tender</td><td>/ˈtendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>温柔的，心软同情的；正式提出</td><td>a tender heart<br>一颗温柔同情的心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a tender heart and cannot bear to see animals suffer.  她心地温软，见不得动物受苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tolerant</td><td>/ˈtɑːlərənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，容忍的</td><td>tolerant of criticism<br>能容忍批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good editor is tolerant of odd but honest writing.  好编辑对古怪却真诚的文字是宽容的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 harsh</td><td>/hɑːrʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的</td><td>harsh criticism<br>严厉的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge's harsh sentence surprised even the prosecutors in the courtroom.  法官严厉的判决连法庭上的检察官都感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 merciless</td><td>/ˈmɜːrsələs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫无仁慈的，无情的</td><td>a merciless critic<br>毫不留情的批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The merciless summer sun beat down on the empty streets.  无情的夏日阳光炙烤着空荡的街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 severe</td><td>/səˈvɪr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的；严重的</td><td>a severe penalty<br>严厉的处罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge imposed a severe penalty on the repeat offender.  法官对累犯施以严厉处罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 strict</td><td>/ˈstrɪkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，严厉的</td><td>strict rules<br>严格的规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her parents were strict about homework and bedtime.  她父母对作业和睡觉时间很严格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lethal</b></td><td>/ˈliːθl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非常有害的，致命的</td><td>a lethal dose<br>致命的剂量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even a small dose of this drug can be lethal to children.  即使少量这种药对儿童也可能致命。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baleful</td><td>/ˈbeɪlfl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的</td><td>a baleful influence<br>有害的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His baleful influence on the young players worried the coach.  他对年轻球员的有害影响让教练担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deadly</td><td>/ˈdedli/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>十足的，完全的；致命的</td><td>deadly serious<br>极其严肃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was deadly serious about quitting her job.  她对辞职的事完全是认真的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deathly</td><td>/ˈdeθli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>死一般的，致命的</td><td>deathly silence<br>死一般的寂静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A deathly silence fell over the courtroom.  法庭上笼罩着死一般的寂静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fatal</td><td>/ˈfeɪtəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>致命的，灾难性的</td><td>a fatal mistake<br>致命的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One fatal mistake in the calculation doomed the launch.  计算中一个致命错误注定了发射失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mortal</td><td>/ˈmɔːrtəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>终将死亡的，凡人的；致命的</td><td>mortal beings<br>凡人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The play reminds us that even great kings are mortal men.  这部剧提醒我们连伟大的国王也是凡人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 murderous</td><td>/ˈmɜːrdərəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>残忍的，杀气腾腾的</td><td>a murderous rage<br>杀气腾腾的怒火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He shot her a murderous look and left.  他向她投去杀气腾腾的一瞥便离开了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pestilent</td><td>/ˈpɛstələnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引起瘟疫的；有害的</td><td>pestilent swamp air<br>招致瘟疫的沼气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Settlers blamed the pestilent swamp air for the summer fevers.  移民把夏季的热病归咎于招致瘟疫的沼气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 terminal</td><td>/ˈtɜːrmənəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>晚期的，致命的</td><td>a terminal illness<br>不治之症</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic specializes in caring for terminal patients.  这家诊所专门照护晚期病人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vital</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满活力的；极重要的</td><td>full of vital energy<br>充满生命活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise keeps older people vital and mentally alert.  经常锻炼使老年人保持活力和头脑清醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 innocuous</td><td>/ɪˈnɑːkjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的；不会引起敌意的</td><td>an innocuous question<br>一个无恶意的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What seemed an innocuous question turned out to reveal her deepest secret.  看似无恶意的问题最终却揭露了她最深的秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lethargic</b></td><td>/ləˈθɑːrdʒɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没精打采的，行动迟缓的</td><td>feel lethargic all day<br>整天没精打采</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat left everyone lethargic and unwilling to move before evening.  酷热让所有人都没精打采，天黑前都不愿动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dull</td><td>/dʌl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的；乏味的</td><td>a dull student<br>迟钝的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lecture was so dull that half the class slept.  讲座乏味得让一半学生睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inert</td><td>/ɪˈnɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（人）懒惰缺乏活力的；（物品）惰性的</td><td>an inert gas<br>惰性气体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Helium is an inert gas, so it rarely reacts with other elements.  氦是一种惰性气体，因此很少与其他元素发生反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quiescent</td><td>/kwiˈesnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>平静的，静止的</td><td>a quiescent period<br>静止期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The volcano has remained quiescent for more than two hundred years.  这座火山已经平静了两百多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sluggish</td><td>/ˈslʌɡɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>行动迟缓的，懒散的</td><td>feel sluggish all morning<br>整个上午都懒洋洋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt sluggish all morning and could barely finish his simplest chores.  他整个上午都懒洋洋的，连最简单的杂事都几乎做不完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torpid</td><td>/ˈtɔːrpɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的，无生气的</td><td>a torpid winter afternoon<br>慵懒迟滞的冬日下午</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat left the whole village torpid and unwilling to move.  酷热让整个村子都变得麻木迟钝，不愿动弹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dynamic</td><td>/daɪˈnæmɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有活力的，动态的</td><td>a dynamic leader<br>有活力的领导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city has a dynamic arts scene that keeps changing.  这座城市有着不断变化的活跃艺术圈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 energetic</td><td>/ˌenərˈdʒetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的</td><td>an energetic performance<br>活力四射的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even at seventy she remains remarkably energetic and full of plans.  即使七十岁了，她依然精力充沛，计划满满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 robust</td><td>/roʊˈbʌst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的；强壮的，健康的</td><td>robust good health<br>强健的好身体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains robust enough to work in the garden daily.  他八十岁了，身体依然强健，每天还能在园中劳作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vigorous</td><td>/ˈvɪɡərəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力旺盛的，强有力的</td><td>vigorous exercise<br>剧烈运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors recommend thirty minutes of vigorous exercise daily.  医生建议每天进行三十分钟的高强度运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>levelheaded</b></td><td>/ˌlevlˈhedɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>头脑冷静的，明智的</td><td>a levelheaded response<br>明智冷静的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her levelheaded response to the emergency probably saved several lives that night.  她对紧急情况明智冷静的回应那晚可能救了好几条命。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 informed</td><td>/ɪnˈfɔːrmd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>见识广的，掌握信息的</td><td>make an informed decision<br>做出明智的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters need accurate data to make an informed choice.  选民需要准确的数据才能做出明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 Justified</td><td>/ˈdʒʌstəˌfaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有正当理由的，合理的</td><td>fully justified anger<br>完全合理的愤怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her complaint was justified, and the store refunded the money.  她的投诉有理，商店退了钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 logical</td><td>/ˈlɑːdʒɪkəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>合乎逻辑的，合理的</td><td>a logical conclusion<br>合乎逻辑的结论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Selling the old machines was the logical next step.  卖掉旧机器是顺理成章的下一步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rational</td><td>/ˈræʃnəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>理性的，合理的</td><td>a rational decision<br>理性的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Try to make a rational decision instead of an emotional one.  试着做理性而非情绪化的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reasonable</td><td>/ˈriːzənəbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>合理的，通情达理的</td><td>a reasonable request<br>合理的请求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That seems like a perfectly reasonable request.  那似乎是完全合理的要求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sensible</td><td>/ˈsensəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明智的，合理的</td><td>a sensible choice<br>明智的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing boots was a sensible choice in that mud.  在那种泥地里穿靴子是明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sober</td><td>/ˈsoʊbər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃的；节制的</td><td>a sober assessment<br>冷静的评估</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee offered a sober assessment of the country's economic prospects.  委员会对国家经济前景作出冷静的评估。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 valid</td><td>/ˈvælɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有效的；有根据的</td><td>a valid objection<br>有根据的反对意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your ticket is valid for travel on any weekday this month.  你的票在本月任何工作日出行均有效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 well-founded</td><td>/ˌwɛl ˈfaʊndɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有充分根据的</td><td>a well-founded fear<br>有充分根据的担忧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their fear of another flood proved to be well-founded.  他们对再次洪水的担忧被证明有充分根据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 foolish</td><td>/ˈfuːlɪʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>愚蠢的，傻的</td><td>a foolish mistake<br>愚蠢的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was foolish of him to ignore the warning signs.  他忽视警示标志实在愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>levity</b></td><td>/ˈlevəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻浮</td><td>a moment of levity<br>一时的轻松打趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A moment of levity relieved the tension in the crowded waiting room.  一时的轻松打趣缓解了拥挤候诊室里的紧张气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 facetiousness</del></td><td>/fəˈsiːʃəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不合时宜的诙谐，轻浮</td><td>unwelcome facetiousness<br>不受欢迎的玩笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His facetiousness at the funeral offended nearly everyone present.  他在葬礼上的轻浮玩笑几乎冒犯了所有在场者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 flightiness</del></td><td>/ˈflaɪtinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻浮，反复无常</td><td>youthful flightiness<br>年轻人的轻浮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her flightiness made her a poor choice for the job.  她的轻浮反复使她不适合这份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flippancy</td><td>/ˈflɪpənsi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻率；轻浮无礼</td><td>treat it with flippancy<br>以轻浮态度对待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His flippancy during the memorial upset the grieving family.  他在悼念仪式上的轻浮无礼让悲伤的家人很难受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 frivolousness</del></td><td>/ˈfrɪvələsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻浮，无聊琐碎</td><td>sheer frivolousness<br>纯粹的轻浮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge criticized the frivolousness of the entire lawsuit.  法官批评了整场诉讼的无聊琐碎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 frothiness</del></td><td>/ˈfrɔːθinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻浮，浅薄；起泡沫</td><td>the frothiness of the show<br>这节目的浅薄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics mocked the frothiness of the summer blockbuster.  评论家嘲笑这部暑期大片的浅薄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 silliness</td><td>/ˈsɪlinəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>愚蠢，无聊可笑</td><td>childish silliness<br>孩子气的傻闹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher tolerated a little silliness at the end of class.  老师容许下课前有点傻闹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 earnestness</td><td>/ˈɜːrnəstnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>认真，诚挚</td><td>speak with earnestness<br>诚挚地讲话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He argued his case with disarming earnestness.  他以令人无法抗拒的诚挚陈述自己的理由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gravity</td><td>/ˈɡrævəti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>严肃，庄重；重力</td><td>the gravity of the situation<br>形势的严峻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody seemed to grasp the gravity of the situation.  似乎没人明白形势的严峻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 seriousness</td><td>/ˈsɪriəsnəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>严肃，认真；严重性</td><td>the seriousness of the charge<br>指控的严重性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She explained the seriousness of the charge to her client.  她向委托人说明了指控的严重性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 soberness</del></td><td>/ˈsoʊbərnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>严肃，冷静；清醒</td><td>the soberness of his tone<br>他语气的严肃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soberness of his tone silenced the whole room.  他语气的严肃让整个房间安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 solemnity</td><td>/səˈlemnəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>庄严，严肃</td><td>with due solemnity<br>以应有的庄严</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coronation was conducted with all the solemnity the occasion demanded.  加冕礼以这一场合所要求的全部庄严举行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>liability</b></td><td>/ˌlaɪəˈbɪləti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>责任</td><td>limited liability<br>有限责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract clearly limits the company's liability for accidental damage.  合同明确限制了公司对意外损害承担的责任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accountability</td><td>/əˌkaʊntəˈbɪləti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>问责，责任</td><td>public accountability<br>对公众的问责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters demanded greater accountability from the council.  选民要求议会承担更大责任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 answerability</del></td><td>/ˌænsərəˈbɪləti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>应负责任，须答复</td><td>answerability to voters<br>对选民负有的责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charter spells out the board's answerability to members.  章程明确了董事会对成员应负的责任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 responsibility</td><td>/rɪˌspɑːnsəˈbɪləti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>责任，职责</td><td>take responsibility for<br>为…承担责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took full responsibility for the missed deadline.  她为错过截止日期承担了全部责任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immunity</td><td>/ɪˈmjuːnəti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>豁免；免疫力</td><td>diplomatic immunity<br>外交豁免权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness testified after being granted immunity.  证人在获得豁免后作证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>
